cluster-point

forall([s, f, x], cluster-point(s, f, x) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and x in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forall([m in nn], forsome([n in nn], m <= n and (dist(s))(f(n), x) < eps))))

col-equiv

col-step-row

forall([r, n, b, i], col-step-row(r, n, b, i) iff forall([csj_ in interval(1, n)], i < csj_ implies entry(b, i, csj_) = zero(r)) and (i in interval(1, n) implies (entry(b, i, i) = zero(r) or entry(b, i, i) = one(r)) and (entry(b, i, i) = one(r) implies forall([csj_ in interval(1, n)], not(csj_ = i) implies entry(b, i, csj_) = zero(r)))))

converges

converges-along

forall([s, g, b, p], converges-along(s, g, b, p) iff is-metric-space(s) and g in fun(nn, pts(s)) and p in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([i in nn], i in b implies n <= i implies (dist(s))(g(i), p) <= eps))))

converges-on

forall([s, fam, dseq], converges-on(s, fam, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn], converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, (fam(k))(dseq(m))))))

converges-to

forall([s, f, l], converges-to(s, f, l) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and l in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([n_ in nn], n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(n_), l) <= eps))))

converges-uniformly

forall([s, seq, g], converges-uniformly(s, seq, g) iff is-metric-space(s) and seq in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and g in fun(pts(s), rr) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((seq(k))(x) - g(x)) < eps)))))

cr-reflexivity

def-in-closed

def-list

def-pred-hyp

def-total

eqv-ok

even

fr-cauchy

forall([m, fam, seq], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) iff forall([k in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([i], i in nn and cap <= i implies forall([j], j in nn and cap <= j implies (fam(k))((vadd(m))(seq(i), (vneg(m))(seq(j)))) < eps)))))

fr-conv

forall([m, fam, seq, lim], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim) iff forall([k in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([i], i in nn and cap <= i implies (fam(k))((vadd(m))(seq(i), (vneg(m))(lim))) < eps))))

generates

has-closed-graph

has-deriv-at

forall([f, a, l], has-deriv-at(f, a, l) iff forsome([ice_], pos-rr(ice_) and is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_), restrict(f, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_)), a, l)))

has-deriv-at-within

forall([f, w, x, l], has-deriv-at-within(f, w, x, l) iff restrict(f, w) in fun(w, rr) and x in w and l in rr and forsome([osp_ in fun(w, rr)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, w), rr-ms, osp_, x) and osp_(x) = l and forall([ost_ in w], f(ost_) - f(x) = osp_(ost_) * (ost_ - x))))

has-dir-deriv

has-div-remainder

forall([s, dg], has-div-remainder(s, dg) iff forall([a_ in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a_ = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b)))))

has-fip

has-inverses

improper-int-converges-to

is-algebra-of-sets

forall([omega, ca], is-algebra-of-sets(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and forsome([a_], a_ in ca) and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([a_ in ca, b_ in ca], union(a_, b_) in ca) and forall([a_ in ca, b_ in ca], intersection(a_, b_) in ca))

is-analytic-at

forall([anlf_, anlu_, anla_], is-analytic-at(anlf_, anlu_, anla_) iff is-open(cc-ms, anlu_) and anlf_ in fun(anlu_, cc) and anla_ in anlu_ and forsome([anlcf_ in fun(nn, cc), anlr_ in rr], 0 < anlr_ and anlr_ < cps-radius(anlcf_) and ball(cc-ms, anla_, anlr_) subset anlu_ and forall([anlz_ in ball(cc-ms, anla_, anlr_)], anlf_(anlz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(anlk_, nn, anlcf_(anlk_) * (anlz_ - anla_) ^ anlk_)))))

is-antiderivable

is-antiderivative

is-antisymmetric-op

is-associative

forall([op, crr], is-associative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], op(op(u, v), w) = op(u, op(v, w))))

is-bounded-linear-functional

forall([m, f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) iff is-linear-functional(m, f) and forsome([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_))))

is-bounded-linear-functional-on

is-bounded-metric-space

is-caratheodory-measurable

forall([omega, mu, es], is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es) iff es subset omega and forall([a_ in power(omega)], mu(a_) = eplus(mu(intersection(a_, es)), mu(difference(a_, es)))))

is-cauchy-seq

forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], n <= m and n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(m), f(n_)) <= eps))))

is-cc-diff-at

forall([g, a, l], is-cc-diff-at(g, a, l) iff g in fun(rr, cc) and a in rr and l in cc and is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(sv_, rr, real-part(g(sv_))), a, real-part(l)) and is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(sv_, rr, imag-part(g(sv_))), a, imag-part(l)))

is-cc-linear-functional

forall([v, f], is-cc-linear-functional(v, f) iff f in fun(vec(v), cc) and forall([hlx_ in vec(v), hly_ in vec(v)], f((vadd(v))(hlx_, hly_)) = f(hlx_) + f(hly_)) and forall([hlc_ in cc, hlx_ in vec(v)], f((act(v))(hlc_, hlx_)) = hlc_ * f(hlx_)))

is-chain

forall([grd, porel, ch], is-chain(grd, porel, ch) iff ch subset grd and forall([x in ch, y in ch], [x, y] in porel or [y, x] in porel))

is-closed

forall([s, a], is-closed(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and is-open(s, complement-in(pts(s), a)))

is-column-step

forall([r, m, n, b], is-column-step(r, m, n, b) iff forall([csi_ in interval(1, m), csj_ in interval(1, n)], csi_ < csj_ implies entry(b, csi_, csj_) = zero(r)) and forall([csi_ in interval(1, m)], csi_ in interval(1, n) implies entry(b, csi_, csi_) = zero(r) or entry(b, csi_, csi_) = one(r)))

is-commutative

forall([op, crr], is-commutative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr], op(u, v) = op(v, u)))

is-compact

forall([s], is-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([c], is-open-cover(s, c) implies forsome([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f))))

is-compact-t

forall([s], is-compact-t(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forall([c], forall([u in c], u in opens(s)) implies big-union(u, c, u) == pts(s) implies forsome([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn and big-union(u, f, u) == pts(s))))

is-complete

forall([s], is-complete(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies converges(s, f)))

is-complete-measure

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-complete-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-measure(omega, ca, mu) and forall([nls_], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, nls_) implies nls_ in ca))

is-concat-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-concat-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sal_ in tuples(msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_)))], length(sas_) = succ(length(sal_)) and forall([sai_ in nn], 1 <= sai_ implies sai_ <= length(sal_) implies nth(sai_, sas_) = [received, nth(sai_, sal_)]) and nth(succ(length(sal_)), sas_) = [sent, lst(sal_)]))

is-connected

forall([cns_, cna_], is-connected(cns_, cna_) iff is-metric-space(cns_) and cna_ subset pts(cns_) and forall([cnu_], is-open(cns_, cnu_) implies forall([cnv_], is-open(cns_, cnv_) implies cna_ subset union(cnu_, cnv_) implies forall([cnx_ in cna_], not(cnx_ in cnu_ and cnx_ in cnv_)) implies forsome([cnx_ in cna_], cnx_ in cnu_) implies not(forsome([cnx_ in cna_], cnx_ in cnv_)))))

is-cont-lin

forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m1)), lim in vec(m1)], fr-conv(m1, fam1, seq, lim) implies fr-conv(m2, fam2, vnb-lambda(i, nn, tt(seq(i))), tt(lim))))

is-continuous

forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([a in pts(s)], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a)))

is-continuous-at

forall([s, t, f, a], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and a in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))

is-continuous-on

forall([f, d], is-continuous-on(f, d) iff f in fun(d, rr) and forall([icp_ in d], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, d), rr-ms, f, icp_)))

is-convex-cc

forall([cvu_], is-convex-cc(cvu_) iff cvu_ subset cc and forall([cvx_ in cvu_, cvy_ in cvu_, cvt_ in ccint(0, 1)], cvx_ + cvt_ * (cvy_ - cvx_) in cvu_))

is-convex-subset

forall([v, cs], is-convex-subset(v, cs) iff cs subset vec(v) and forall([sxa_ in cs, sxb_ in cs, sxt_ in rr], 0 <= sxt_ implies sxt_ <= 1 implies (vadd(v))((act(v))(1 - sxt_, sxa_), (act(v))(sxt_, sxb_)) in cs))

is-countable

forall([rpc_], is-countable(rpc_) iff rpc_ in set and (rpc_ = empty-set or forsome([rpen_ in fun(nn, rpc_)], forall([rpy_ in rpc_], forsome([rpn_ in nn], rpy_ = rpen_(rpn_))))))

is-countable-pseudometric-family

forall([fam, ground], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, ground) iff forall([n_ in nn], is-pseudometric-space(fam(n_)) and pts(fam(n_)) == ground))

is-decrypt-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-decrypt-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sak_ in keys(sasp_), sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, (kinv(sasp_))(sak_)], [received, encr(sag_, sak_)], [sent, sag_]]))

is-dense

forall([dns_, dna_], is-dense(dns_, dna_) iff is-metric-space(dns_) and dna_ subset pts(dns_) and pts(dns_) subset closure(dns_, dna_))

is-dense-seq

forall([s, dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([m in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(m)) < eps)))

is-diagonal

forall([a, m, n, d], is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) iff forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(i = j) implies entry(d, i, j) = zero(a)))

is-diff-at

forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and l in rr and forsome([phi in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x in rr], f(x) - f(a) = phi(x) * (x - a))))

is-diff-at-v

forall([m, f, a, l], is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l) iff is-normed-vector-space(m) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and l in vec(m) and forsome([phi in fun(rr, vec(m))], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x_ in rr], (vadd(m))(f(x_), (vneg(m))(f(a))) = (act(m))(x_ - a, phi(x_)))))

is-diff-on

forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) iff is-normed-field(k) and is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u) and f in fun(u, carr(k)) and a in u and l in carr(k) and forsome([phi in fun(u, carr(k))], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(k), u), nf-metric-space(k), phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x in u], (add(k))(f(x), (neg(k))(f(a))) = (mul(k))(phi(x), (add(k))(x, (neg(k))(a))))))

is-dir-diff-at

forall([m, f, a, eta, dl], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) iff is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), 0, dl))

is-distributive

forall([addop, mulop, crr], is-distributive(addop, mulop, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], mulop(u, addop(v, w)) = addop(mulop(u, v), mulop(u, w)) and mulop(addop(u, v), w) = addop(mulop(u, w), mulop(v, w))))

is-dynkin-system

forall([omega, dd], is-dynkin-system(omega, dd) iff omega in set and dd subset power(omega) and omega in dd and forall([dya_ in dd, dyb_ in dd], dya_ subset dyb_ implies complement-in(dyb_, dya_) in dd) and forall([dyf_ in fun(nn, dd)], forall([dyn_ in nn], dyf_(dyn_) subset dyf_(succ(dyn_))) implies big-union(dyk_, nn, dyf_(dyk_)) in dd))

is-elementary

forall([r, n, e], is-elementary(r, n, e) iff forsome([elk_, ell_], elk_ in interval(1, n) and ell_ in interval(1, n) and not(elk_ = ell_) and e = elem-f(r, n, elk_, ell_)) or forsome([elr_, elk_, ell_], elr_ in carr(r) and elk_ in interval(1, n) and ell_ in interval(1, n) and not(elk_ = ell_) and e = elem-g(r, n, elr_, elk_, ell_)) or forsome([elr_, elk_], elr_ in carr(r) and forsome([els_ in carr(r)], (mul(r))(elr_, els_) = one(r) and (mul(r))(els_, elr_) = one(r)) and elk_ in interval(1, n) and e = elem-h(r, n, elr_, elk_)))

is-encrypt-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-encrypt-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sak_ in keys(sasp_), sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sak_], [received, sag_], [sent, encr(sag_, sak_)]]))

is-entry-point

forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssi_], is-entry-point(ssp_, ssn_, ssi_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) and node-content(ssp_, ssn_) in ssi_ and forall([ssm_], same-strand-before(ssp_, ssm_, ssn_) implies not(node-content(ssp_, ssm_) in ssi_)))

is-eps-cauchy-seq

forall([s, eps, y], is-eps-cauchy-seq(s, eps, y) iff is-metric-space(s) and y in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], (dist(s))(y(m), y(n_)) <= eps))

is-equicontinuous

forall([s, t, fam], is-equicontinuous(s, t, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), pts(t))) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([del], pos-rr(del) and forall([k in nn, y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) < del implies (dist(t))((fam(k))(x), (fam(k))(y)) < eps))))

is-equivalence

forall([rho, crr], is-equivalence(rho, crr) iff rho in power(cartesian(crr, crr)) and forall([u in crr], [u, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr], [u, v] in rho implies [v, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], [u, v] in rho and [v, w] in rho implies [u, w] in rho))

is-finite-cover

forall([c, a], is-finite-cover(c, a) iff c in set and card(c) in nn and a subset big-union(u, c, u))

is-finite-dimensional

forall([m], is-finite-dimensional(m) iff is-vector-space(m) and is-noetherian(m))

is-flush-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-flush-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sag_]]))

is-fr-bounded

forall([m, fam, aa], is-fr-bounded(m, fam, aa) iff aa subset vec(m) and forall([k in nn], forsome([bd in rr], forall([y in aa], (fam(k))(y) <= bd))))

is-fr-equicont

forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, ix, tf], is-fr-equicont(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, ix, tf) iff tf in fun(ix, fun(vec(m1), vec(m2))) and forall([k in nn], forsome([nb in nn, ck], pos-rr(ck) and forall([i in ix, x in vec(m1), r in rr], forall([j in nn], j <= nb implies (fam1(j))(x) <= r) implies (fam2(k))((tf(i))(x)) <= ck * r))))

is-frechet-structure

forall([fld, m, fam], is-frechet-structure(fld, m, fam) iff is-seminorm-family(fld, m, fam) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m))], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) implies forsome([lim in vec(m)], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim))))

is-fubini-set

forall([xs, ca, mu, ys, cb, nu, cs], is-fubini-set(xs, ca, mu, ys, cb, nu, cs) iff is-measurable-fn(xs, ca, x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, cs)) and is-measurable-fn(ys, cb, y-section-measure(xs, ys, mu, cs)) and integral(xs, ca, mu, x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, cs)) = integral(ys, cb, nu, y-section-measure(xs, ys, mu, cs)))

is-gauge-countable

forall([s], is-gauge-countable(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([fam], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, pts(s)) and pseudo-gauge-top(fam, pts(s)) == s))

is-group-norm

forall([nm, op, invop, unit, crr], is-group-norm(nm, op, invop, unit, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([u in crr], 0 <= nm(u) and (nm(u) = 0 iff u = unit) and nm(invop(u)) = nm(u) and forall([v in crr], nm(op(u, v)) <= nm(u) + nm(v))))

is-hausdorff

forall([s], is-hausdorff(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies not(a == b) implies forsome([u, v], u in opens(s) and v in opens(s) and a in u and b in v and intersection(u, v) == empty-set)))

is-hilbert-space

forall([v], is-hilbert-space(v) iff is-complex-inner-product-space(v) and is-complete(ip-metric-space(v)))

is-homeomorphism

forall([hmes_, hmet_, hmef_], is-homeomorphism(hmes_, hmet_, hmef_) iff hmef_ in bijection(pts(hmes_), pts(hmet_)) and is-continuous(hmes_, hmet_, hmef_) and is-continuous(hmet_, hmes_, inverse-bij(hmef_, pts(hmes_), pts(hmet_))))

is-ideal

forall([s, i], is-ideal(s, i) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and i subset carr(s) and zero(s) in i and forall([a in i, b in i], (add(s))(a, b) in i) and forall([a in i], (neg(s))(a) in i) and forall([r in carr(s), a in i], (mul(s))(r, a) in i))

is-ideal-in

forall([i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_], is-ideal-in(i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_) iff i_ subset carr_ and zero_ in i_ and forall([a, b], a in i_ implies b in i_ implies add_(a, b) in i_) and forall([a in i_], neg_(a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(x_, a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(a, x_) in i_))

is-identity

forall([op, unit, crr], is-identity(op, unit, crr) iff forall([u in crr], op(unit, u) = u and op(u, unit) = u))

is-init-strand

forall([nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-init-strand(nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_) iff nla_ in names(nlp_) and nlb_ in names(nlp_) and nlm_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and nln_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and not(nlm_ in names(nlp_)) and not(nlm_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and not(nln_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and nls_ = [[sent, encr(lst([nlm_, nla_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))], [received, encr(lst([nlm_, nln_, nlb_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nla_))], [sent, encr(nln_, (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))]])

is-integrable

forall([omega, ca, mu, f], is-integrable(omega, ca, mu, f) iff is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and integral(omega, ca, mu, pos-part(omega, f)) in rr and integral(omega, ca, mu, neg-part(omega, f)) in rr)

is-invertible-mat

forall([a, n, u], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) iff u in mat(n, n, carr(a)) and forsome([v in mat(n, n, carr(a))], matmul(a, u, v) = identmat(a, n) and matmul(a, v, u) = identmat(a, n)))

is-isometric-op

forall([v, jo], is-isometric-op(v, jo) iff forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (rip(v))(jo(x_), jo(y_)) = (rip(v))(x_, y_)))

is-isometry

forall([s, t, f], is-isometry(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(t))(f(x), f(y)) = (dist(s))(x, y)))

is-k-linear

forall([m, f], is-k-linear(m, f) iff is-module(m) and f in fun(vec(m), carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))

is-k-linear-on

forall([m, s, f], is-k-linear-on(m, s, f) iff is-module(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and f in fun(s, carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in s, y in s], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in s], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))

is-left-limit

forall([f, x, l], is-left-limit(f, x, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and x in rr and l in rr and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([delta_], pos-rr(delta_) and forall([t_], t_ in rr and x - delta_ <= t_ and t_ < x implies abs(f(t_) - l) <= eps_))))

is-left-limit-within

forall([rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_], is-left-limit-within(rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_) iff rpw_ subset rr and rpf_ in fun(rpw_, rr) and rpx_ in rr and rpl_ in rr and forall([rple_], pos-rr(rple_) implies forsome([rpld_], pos-rr(rpld_) and forall([rplt_ in rpw_], rpx_ - rpld_ < rplt_ implies rplt_ < rpx_ implies abs(rpf_(rplt_) - rpl_) < rple_))))

is-limit-along

forall([lmas_, lmat_, lmaa_, lmaf_, lmax_, lmay_], is-limit-along(lmas_, lmat_, lmaa_, lmaf_, lmax_, lmay_) iff is-metric-space(lmas_) and is-metric-space(lmat_) and lmay_ in pts(lmat_) and forall([lmae_], pos-rr(lmae_) implies forsome([lmad_], pos-rr(lmad_) and forall([lmaz_ in lmaa_], (dist(lmas_))(lmax_, lmaz_) <= lmad_ implies (dist(lmat_))(lmaf_(lmaz_), lmay_) <= lmae_))))

is-linear-functional

forall([m, f], is-linear-functional(m, f) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))

is-linear-functional-on

forall([m, s, f], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) iff f in fun(s, rr) and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in s], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))

is-linear-map

forall([m1, m2, tt], is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) iff is-module(m1) and is-module(m2) and scal(m1) = scal(m2) and tt in fun(vec(m1), vec(m2)) and forall([x in vec(m1), y in vec(m1)], tt((vadd(m1))(x, y)) = (vadd(m2))(tt(x), tt(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m1)), x in vec(m1)], tt((act(m1))(lam, x)) = (act(m2))(lam, tt(x))))

is-local-max-at

forall([f, d, lmt_], is-local-max-at(f, d, lmt_) iff f in fun(d, rr) and lmt_ in d and forsome([lma_], pos-rr(lma_) and forall([lmx_ in d], abs(lmx_ - lmt_) <= lma_ implies f(lmx_) <= f(lmt_))))

is-local-min-at

forall([f, d, lmt_], is-local-min-at(f, d, lmt_) iff f in fun(d, rr) and lmt_ in d and forsome([lma_], pos-rr(lma_) and forall([lmx_ in d], abs(lmx_ - lmt_) <= lma_ implies f(lmt_) <= f(lmx_))))

is-lower-triangular

forall([a, n, d], is-lower-triangular(a, n, d) iff forall([ltu_, ltv_], ltu_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ in interval(1, n) implies ltu_ < ltv_ implies entry(d, ltu_, ltv_) = zero(a)))

is-maximal

forall([grd, porel, mx], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx) iff mx in grd and forall([y in grd], [mx, y] in porel implies [y, mx] in porel))

is-meager

forall([s, a], is-meager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and forsome([ee in fun(nn, power(pts(s)))], forall([n in nn], is-nowhere-dense(s, ee(n))) and a subset big-union(n, nn, ee(n))))

is-measurable-fn

forall([omega, ca, f], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and f in fun(omega, rr-pos-star) and forall([alpha_ in rr], {x_ in omega: alpha_ < f(x_)} in ca))

is-measurable-map

forall([omega, ca, omega2, cb, f], is-measurable-map(omega, ca, omega2, cb, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and is-sigma-algebra(omega2, cb) and f in fun(omega, omega2) and forall([b_ in cb], {x_ in omega: f(x_) in b_} in ca))

is-measure

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and mu in fun(ca, rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies not(i_ = j_) implies intersection(f_(i_), f_(j_)) = empty-set) implies mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) = esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))

is-metric

forall([dst, crr], is-metric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and (dst(u, v) = 0 implies u = v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))

is-metric-outer-measure

forall([m, mu], is-metric-outer-measure(m, mu) iff is-metric-space(m) and is-outer-measure(pts(m), mu) and forall([moa_ in power(pts(m)), mob_ in power(pts(m))], not(set-dist(m, moa_, mob_) = 0) implies mu(union(moa_, mob_)) = eplus(mu(moa_), mu(mob_))))

is-monotone-class

forall([omega, mc], is-monotone-class(omega, mc) iff omega in set and mc subset power(omega) and forall([mcf_ in fun(nn, mc)], forall([mcn_ in nn], mcf_(mcn_) subset mcf_(succ(mcn_))) implies big-union(mck_, nn, mcf_(mck_)) in mc) and forall([mcg_ in fun(nn, mc)], forall([mcm_ in nn], mcg_(succ(mcm_)) subset mcg_(mcm_)) implies complement-in(omega, big-union(mcj_, nn, complement-in(omega, mcg_(mcj_)))) in mc))

is-ms-sequence

forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) iff forall([n in nn], is-metric-space(ms(n))))

is-noetherian

forall([m], is-noetherian(m) iff is-module(m) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(vec(m)))], forall([n_ in nn], is-submodule(m, f_(n_))) and forall([n_ in nn], f_(n_) subset f_(succ(n_))) implies forsome([k_ in nn], forall([n_], n_ in nn and k_ <= n_ implies f_(n_) = f_(k_)))))

is-nonmeager

forall([s, a], is-nonmeager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and not(is-meager(s, a)))

is-nontrivially-valued

forall([fld], is-nontrivially-valued(fld) iff is-normed-field(fld) and forsome([c in carr(fld)], 1 < (fnrm(fld))(c) and forsome([b in carr(fld)], (mul(fld))(c, b) = one(fld))))

is-norm

forall([nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr], is-norm(nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([a in crr], 0 <= nm(a) and (nm(a) = 0 iff a = zr) and forall([b in crr], nm(mulop(a, b)) = nm(a) * nm(b) and nm(addop(a, b)) <= nm(a) + nm(b))))

is-normally-convergent

forall([u, cpsxs_], is-normally-convergent(u, cpsxs_) iff forall([cpsi_ in nn], cc-sup-norm(u(cpsi_), cpsxs_) in rr) and series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsi_, nn, cc-sup-norm(u(cpsi_), cpsxs_))))

is-nowhere-dense

forall([s, a], is-nowhere-dense(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and interior(s, closure(s, a)) = empty-set)

is-nsl-space

forall([nlp_], is-nsl-space(nlp_) iff is-pk-space(nlp_) and forall([nli_ in ids(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))], nli_ in adv-ids(pk-inf(nlp_)) or forsome([nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-init-strand(nlp_, (strand-map(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))))(nli_), nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_)) or forsome([nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-resp-strand(nlp_, (strand-map(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))))(nli_), nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_))))

is-null-set

forall([omega, ca, mu, ns], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, ns) iff forsome([nla_ in ca], ns subset nla_ and mu(nla_) = 0))

is-open

forall([s, u], is-open(s, u) iff is-metric-space(s) and u subset pts(s) and forall([y in u], forsome([r], pos-rr(r) and ball(s, y, r) subset u)))

is-open-cover

forall([s, c], is-open-cover(s, c) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([u in c], is-open(s, u)) and big-union(u, c, u) == pts(s))

is-open-gauge

forall([fam, ground, u], is-open-gauge(fam, ground, u) iff u subset ground and forall([y in u], forsome([k_ in nn, r], pos-rr(r) and forall([x in ground], forall([n_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies (dist(fam(n_)))(y, x) < r) implies x in u))))

is-open-lin-map

forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-open-lin-map(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([n in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn, eps_], pos-rr(eps_) and fr-ball(m2, fam2, n_, eps_) subset image(tt, fr-ball(m1, fam1, n, eps)))))

is-orthogonal-projection

forall([v, ks, pk], is-orthogonal-projection(v, ks, pk) iff pk in fun(vec(v), vec(v)) and forall([opz_ in vec(v)], pk(opz_) in ks and (vadd(v))(opz_, (vneg(v))(pk(opz_))) in perp(v, ks)))

is-outer-measure

forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) iff omega in set and mu in fun(power(omega), rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([a_, b_], a_ in power(omega) implies b_ in power(omega) implies a_ subset b_ implies mu(a_) <= mu(b_)) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(omega))], mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) <= esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))

is-partial-order

forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) iff grd in set and porel in set and forall([x in grd], [x, x] in porel) and forall([x in grd, y in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, x] in porel implies x = y) and forall([x in grd, y in grd, z in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, z] in porel implies [x, z] in porel))

is-partition

forall([p, n, a, b], is-partition(p, n, a, b) iff n in nn and 1 <= n and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and p in fun(nn, rr) and p(0) = a and p(n) = b and forall([i_, j_], i_ in interval(0, n) and j_ in interval(0, n) and i_ < j_ implies p(i_) < p(j_)))

is-path

forall([pgam, a, b], is-path(pgam, a, b) iff a in rr and b in rr and a < b and pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) and forall([pax_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), cc-ms, pgam, pax_)))

is-pi-system

forall([pp], is-pi-system(pp) iff forall([pya_ in pp, pyb_ in pp], intersection(pya_, pyb_) in pp))

is-piecewise-continuous

forall([f, a, b], is-piecewise-continuous(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forsome([n_, p_], is-partition(p_, n_, a, b) and forall([i_], i_ in interval(0, n_) and i_ < n_ implies forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_(i_) < t_ and t_ < p_(succ(i_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, t_)))))

is-positive-functional

forall([s, lam], is-positive-functional(s, lam) iff lam in fun(cont-fns(s), rr) and forall([pff_ in cont-fns(s), pfg_ in cont-fns(s)], lam(vnb-lambda(sx_, pts(s), pff_(sx_) + pfg_(sx_))) = lam(pff_) + lam(pfg_)) and forall([pfc_ in rr, pff_ in cont-fns(s)], lam(vnb-lambda(sx_, pts(s), pfc_ * pff_(sx_))) = pfc_ * lam(pff_)) and forall([pff_ in cont-fns(s)], forall([pfx_ in pts(s)], 0 <= pff_(pfx_)) implies 0 <= lam(pff_)))

is-prec-closed

forall([spc_, snd_, ssd_, srl_], is-prec-closed(spc_, snd_, ssd_, srl_) iff forall([ssx_, ssy_], [ssx_, ssy_] in ssd_ implies [ssx_, ssy_] in srl_) and forall([ssx_ in snd_, ssy_ in snd_], same-strand-before(spc_, ssx_, ssy_) implies [ssx_, ssy_] in srl_) and forall([ssx_, ssy_, ssz_], [ssx_, ssy_] in srl_ implies [ssy_, ssz_] in srl_ implies [ssx_, ssz_] in srl_))

is-premeasure

forall([omega, cls, pm], is-premeasure(omega, cls, pm) iff omega in set and cls subset power(omega) and empty-set in cls and pm in fun(cls, rr-pos-star) and pm(empty-set) = 0)

is-primitive

forall([rpg_, rpf_, rpa_, rpb_], is-primitive(rpg_, rpf_, rpa_, rpb_) iff rpa_ in rr and rpb_ in rr and rpa_ < rpb_ and rpg_ in fun(ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rr) and rpf_ in fun(ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rr) and is-continuous-on(rpg_, ccint(rpa_, rpb_)) and forsome([rps_], is-countable(rps_) and rps_ subset ccint(rpa_, rpb_) and forall([rpt_ in ooint(rpa_, rpb_)], not(rpt_ in rps_) implies has-deriv-at(rpg_, rpt_, rpf_(rpt_)))))

is-pseudometric

forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))

is-ptwise-cauchy

forall([s, fam], is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps)))))

is-pw-antiderivable

forall([pphi_, a, b], is-pw-antiderivable(pphi_, a, b) iff forsome([paw_], is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b)))

is-pw-continuous-on

forall([pphi_, a, b], is-pw-continuous-on(pphi_, a, b) iff a in rr and b in rr and a < b and pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) and forsome([pas_ in set], card(pas_) in nn and pas_ subset ccint(a, b) and forall([pat_ in ooint(a, b)], not(pat_ in pas_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), rr-ms, pphi_, pat_))))

is-r-net

forall([s, f, a, r], is-r-net(s, f, a, r) iff f subset a and forall([p in a], forsome([c in f], (dist(s))(c, p) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, p) = r))))

is-real-hilbert-space

forall([v], is-real-hilbert-space(v) iff is-pre-hilbert-space(v) and is-complete(sip-metric-space(v)))

is-real-linear-op

forall([v, jo], is-real-linear-op(v, jo) iff jo in fun(vec(v), vec(v)) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], jo((vadd(v))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(v))(jo(x_), jo(y_))) and forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(v)], jo((act(v))(a_, x_)) = (act(v))(a_, jo(x_))))

is-real-measurable-fn

forall([omega, ca, f], is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) iff f in fun(omega, rr) and is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, pos-part(omega, f)) and is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, neg-part(omega, f)))

is-real-orthogonal-projection

forall([v, ks, pk], is-real-orthogonal-projection(v, ks, pk) iff pk in fun(vec(v), vec(v)) and forall([sop_ in vec(v)], pk(sop_) in ks and (vadd(v))(sop_, (vneg(v))(pk(sop_))) in sip-perp(v, ks)))

is-received

forall([ssp_, ssn_], is-received(ssp_, ssn_) iff node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) = received)

is-regular-node

forall([sai_, san_], is-regular-node(sai_, san_) iff not(nth(2, san_) in adv-ids(sai_)))

is-regulated

forall([f, a, b], is-regulated(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], (x_ < b implies forsome([l_], is-right-limit(f, x_, l_))) and (a < x_ implies forsome([l_], is-left-limit(f, x_, l_)))))

is-regulated-on

forall([rpf_, rpa_, rpb_], is-regulated-on(rpf_, rpa_, rpb_) iff rpa_ in rr and rpb_ in rr and rpa_ < rpb_ and rpf_ in fun(ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rr) and forall([rpx_ in ccint(rpa_, rpb_)], rpx_ < rpb_ implies forsome([rpl_], is-right-limit-within(rpf_, ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rpx_, rpl_))) and forall([rpx_ in ccint(rpa_, rpb_)], rpa_ < rpx_ implies forsome([rpl_], is-left-limit-within(rpf_, ccint(rpa_, rpb_), rpx_, rpl_))))

is-resp-strand

forall([nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_], is-resp-strand(nlp_, nls_, nla_, nlb_, nlm_, nln_) iff nla_ in names(nlp_) and nlb_ in names(nlp_) and nlm_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and nln_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_))) and not(nln_ in names(nlp_)) and not(nlm_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and not(nln_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nlp_)))) and nls_ = [[received, encr(lst([nlm_, nla_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))], [sent, encr(lst([nlm_, nln_, nlb_]), (pubkey(nlp_))(nla_))], [received, encr(nln_, (pubkey(nlp_))(nlb_))]])

is-right-limit

forall([f, x, l], is-right-limit(f, x, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and x in rr and l in rr and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([delta_], pos-rr(delta_) and forall([t_], t_ in rr and x < t_ and t_ <= x + delta_ implies abs(f(t_) - l) <= eps_))))

is-right-limit-within

forall([rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_], is-right-limit-within(rpf_, rpw_, rpx_, rpl_) iff rpw_ subset rr and rpf_ in fun(rpw_, rr) and rpx_ in rr and rpl_ in rr and forall([rpe_], pos-rr(rpe_) implies forsome([rpdl_], pos-rr(rpdl_) and forall([rpt_ in rpw_], rpx_ < rpt_ implies rpt_ < rpx_ + rpdl_ implies abs(rpf_(rpt_) - rpl_) < rpe_))))

is-road

forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) iff is-path(pgam, a, b) and dgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) and is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pgam(pat_))), vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pgam(pat_))), vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b))

is-seminorm

forall([fld, m, p], is-seminorm(fld, m, p) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x in vec(m)], 0 <= p(x)) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(x, y)) <= p(x) + p(y)) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], p((act(m))(lam, x)) = (fnrm(fld))(lam) * p(x)))

is-seminorm-family

forall([fld, m, fam], is-seminorm-family(fld, m, fam) iff is-normed-field(fld) and is-module(m) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and fam in fun(nn, fun(vec(m), rr)) and forall([k in nn], is-seminorm(fld, m, fam(k))) and forall([x in vec(m)], not(x = vzero(m)) implies forsome([k in nn], not((fam(k))(x) = 0))))

is-sent

forall([ssp_, ssn_], is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) iff node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) = sent)

is-separable

forall([s], is-separable(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([dseq in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(n)) < eps))))

is-separate-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-separate-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sal_ in tuples(msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_)))], length(sas_) = succ(length(sal_)) and nth(1, sas_) = [received, lst(sal_)] and forall([sai_ in nn], 1 <= sai_ implies sai_ <= length(sal_) implies nth(succ(sai_), sas_) = [sent, nth(sai_, sal_)])))

is-sesquilinear-form

forall([v, sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) iff sqf in fun(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cc) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], sqf((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = sqf(x_, z_) + sqf(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf((cact(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * sqf(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf(y_, x_) = conjugate(sqf(x_, y_))))

is-sigma-algebra

forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and omega in ca and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in ca))

is-sigma-finite

forall([omega, ca, mu], is-sigma-finite(omega, ca, mu) iff forsome([sff_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([sfn_ in nn], mu(sff_(sfn_)) in rr) and big-union(sfk_, nn, sff_(sfk_)) = omega))

is-simple-fn

forall([omega, ca, f], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and card(image(f, omega)) in nn and forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) in rr))

is-square-minus-id

forall([v, jo], is-square-minus-id(v, jo) iff forall([x_ in vec(v)], jo(jo(x_)) = (vneg(v))(x_)))

is-step-fn

forall([f, a, b], is-step-fn(f, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forsome([n_, p_], is-partition(p_, n_, a, b) and forall([i_], i_ in interval(0, n_) and i_ < n_ implies forsome([c_ in rr], forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_(i_) < t_ and t_ < p_(succ(i_)) implies f(t_) = c_)))))

is-strictly-below

forall([porel, x, y], is-strictly-below(porel, x, y) iff [x, y] in porel and not([y, x] in porel))

is-strictly-increasing-on

forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and s subset rr and forall([u in s, v in s], u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))

is-sublinear

forall([m, p], is-sublinear(m, p) iff p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([hsx_ in vec(m), hsy_ in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(hsx_, hsy_)) <= p(hsx_) + p(hsy_)) and forall([hst_ in rr], 0 <= hst_ implies forall([hsx_ in vec(m)], p((act(m))(hst_, hsx_)) = hst_ * p(hsx_))))

is-submodule

forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) iff s subset vec(m) and vzero(m) in s and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s) and forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))

is-subsequence

forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))

is-subspace

forall([m, s], is-subspace(m, s) iff is-submodule(m, s))

is-subterm

forall([smt_, smk_, sma_, smb_], is-subterm(smt_, smk_, sma_, smb_) iff [sma_, smb_] in subterm-rel(smt_, smk_))

is-subterm-closed

forall([smt_, smk_, smr_], is-subterm-closed(smt_, smk_, smr_) iff forall([sma_ in msg-set(smt_, smk_)], [sma_, sma_] in smr_) and forall([sma_, sml_, smi_], sml_ in tuples(msg-set(smt_, smk_)) implies smi_ in nn implies 1 <= smi_ implies smi_ <= length(sml_) implies [sma_, nth(smi_, sml_)] in smr_ implies [sma_, lst(sml_)] in smr_) and forall([sma_, smg_, smy_], smg_ in msg-set(smt_, smk_) implies smy_ in smk_ implies [sma_, smg_] in smr_ implies [sma_, encr(smg_, smy_)] in smr_))

is-summable

forall([grp, f], is-summable(grp, f) iff forsome([r in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r)))

is-taylor-family

forall([d, n, a, b], is-taylor-family(d, n, a, b) iff n in nn and 1 <= n and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and d(n) in fun(ooint(a, b), rr) and forall([tk_ in nn], tk_ < n implies d(tk_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) and is-continuous-on(d(tk_), ccint(a, b)) and forall([tx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(d(tk_), tx_, (d(succ(tk_)))(tx_)))))

is-tee-trace

forall([sasp_, sas_], is-tee-trace(sasp_, sas_) iff forsome([sag_ in msg-set(tokens(sasp_), keys(sasp_))], sas_ = [[received, sag_], [sent, sag_], [sent, sag_]]))

is-text-trace

forall([sasp_, sap_, sas_], is-text-trace(sasp_, sap_, sas_) iff forsome([saa_ in sap_], sas_ = [[sent, saa_]]))

is-token-set

forall([smt_], is-token-set(smt_) iff smt_ in set and forall([smx_ in smt_], not(smx_ in tuples(set))))

is-unif-cauchy

forall([s, fam], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps))))))

is-unif-limit-on

forall([fam, g, a, b], is-unif-limit-on(fam, g, a, b) iff fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)) and g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_], k_ in nn and n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(g(x_) - (fam(k_))(x_)) <= eps_)))))

is-uniformly-continuous

forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))

is-upper-bound

forall([grd, porel, ch, b], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch, b) iff b in grd and forall([x in ch], [x, b] in porel))

is-upper-triangular

forall([a, n, d], is-upper-triangular(a, n, d) iff forall([ltu_, ltv_], ltu_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ in interval(1, n) implies ltv_ < ltu_ implies entry(d, ltu_, ltv_) = zero(a)))

little-o-at

forall([g, a], little-o-at(g, a) iff g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and forsome([eps in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, eps, a) and eps(a) = 0 and forall([x in rr], g(x) = eps(x) * (x - a))))

mat-equiv

forall([a, m, n, c, d], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) iff forsome([u], is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) and forsome([v], is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) and d = matmul(a, matmul(a, u, c), v))))

npe

forall([m, s, f, t, g], npe(m, s, f, t, g) iff is-submodule(m, t) and s subset t and is-linear-functional-on(m, t, g) and extends-on(s, g, f) and forall([w_ in t], abs(g(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_)))

null-rr-seq

forall([rad], null-rr-seq(rad) iff rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([k], k in nn and n <= k implies rad(k) <= eps))))

occurs-in-space

forall([ssp_, ssu_], occurs-in-space(ssp_, ssu_) iff forsome([ssn_ in nodes(ssp_)], is-subterm(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_), ssu_, node-content(ssp_, ssn_))))

odd

forall([x], odd(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k + 1))

originates

forall([ssp_, ssu_, ssn_], originates(ssp_, ssu_, ssn_) iff is-entry-point(ssp_, ssn_, {ssw_ in msg-set(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_)): is-subterm(tokens(ssp_), keys(ssp_), ssu_, ssw_)}))

pointwise-bounded

forall([s, fam], pointwise-bounded(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s)], forsome([bd in rr], forall([k in nn], abs((fam(k))(x)) <= bd))))

pos-rr

forall([r], pos-rr(r) iff r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r))

pred-code

forall([mcpk_], pred-code(mcpk_) iff mcpk_ = 7 or mcpk_ = 9 or mcpk_ = 11 or mcpk_ = 13)

ps-absolutely-converges-at

forall([coef, x], ps-absolutely-converges-at(coef, x) iff ps-converges-at(vnb-lambda(n, nn, abs(coef(n))), abs(x)))

ps-converges-at

forall([coef, x], ps-converges-at(coef, x) iff converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k))))

ps-converges-to-at

forall([coef, x, l], ps-converges-to-at(coef, x, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k)), l))

ps-ratio-limit

forall([coef, l], ps-ratio-limit(coef, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(n, nn, abs(coef(succ(n)) * recip(coef(n)))), l))

ptwise-le

forall([omega, f, g], ptwise-le(omega, f, g) iff forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) <= g(x_)))

rel-big-union-mem-elim

forall([mit_, mihs_, mic_], rel-big-union-mem-elim(mit_, mihs_, mic_) iff mic_ in seq-set and forsome([mig_, mif_, mia_, mib_, mix_, mie_], mic_ = [mig_, mif_] and enc-in(mix_, enc-bu(mia_, mib_)) in mig_ and mie_ in nn and not(mie_ in union(ctx-fv(mig_), expr-fv(mif_))) and mihs_ = [[ctx-add(ctx-add(difference(mig_, pair(enc-in(mix_, enc-bu(mia_, mib_)), enc-in(mix_, enc-bu(mia_, mib_)))), enc-in(efvar(mie_), mia_)), enc-in(mix_, expr-open(mib_, 0, efvar(mie_)))), mif_]]))

rel-detach

forall([mct_, mchs_, mcc_], rel-detach(mct_, mchs_, mcc_) iff mcc_ in seq-set and forsome([mcg_, mcf_, mcp_, mcq_], mcc_ = [mcg_, mcf_] and enc-imp(mcp_, mcq_) in mcg_ and mcp_ in mcg_ and mchs_ = [[ctx-add(mcg_, mcq_), mcf_]]))

rel-free

forall([md, n, u], rel-free(md, n, u) iff forall([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], lincomb(md, n, c_, u) = vzero(md) implies forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c_, 1, j_) = zero(scal(md)))))

rel-intersection-intro

rm-address

forall([s, dseq, es, y], rm-address(s, dseq, es, y) iff y in pts(s) and forall([ran_ in nn], (es(ran_) = 1 implies y in rm-ball(s, dseq, ran_)) and (not(es(ran_) = 1) implies y in rm-shell(s, dseq, ran_))))

forall([mrt_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-rel-l(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) iff forsome([mrg_, mrh_, mra_, mrb_, mrc_], mrx_ = [2, mrg_, eapp(mrh_, [mra_, mrc_]), eapp(mrh_, [mrb_, mrc_])] and mrh_ in nn and 7 <= mrh_ and mrh_ <= 9 and mrc_ in clt and [1, mrg_, mra_, mrb_] in mrs_))

rw-rel-r

forall([mrt_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-rel-r(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) iff forsome([mrg_, mrh_, mra_, mrb_, mrc_], mrx_ = [2, mrg_, eapp(mrh_, [mrc_, mra_]), eapp(mrh_, [mrc_, mrb_])] and mrh_ in nn and 7 <= mrh_ and mrh_ <= 9 and mrc_ in clt and [1, mrg_, mra_, mrb_] in mrs_))

rw-rule

forall([mrt_, mrk_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-rule(mrt_, mrk_, mrs_, mrx_) iff 1 <= mrk_ and rw-top(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-not(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-and-l(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-and-r(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-or-l(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-or-r(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-imp-l(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-imp-r(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-iff-l(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-iff-r(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-rel-l(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 1 <= mrk_ and rw-rel-r(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 2 <= mrk_ and rw-all(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 2 <= mrk_ and rw-ex(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 4 <= mrk_ and rw-pred(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 4 <= mrk_ and rw-tapp(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 5 <= mrk_ and rw-bind-dom(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 6 <= mrk_ and rw-sep-body(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 6 <= mrk_ and rw-tbind-body(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 7 <= mrk_ and rw-desc-body(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 3 <= mrk_ and rw-if-cond(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 3 <= mrk_ and rw-if-then(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) or 3 <= mrk_ and rw-if-else(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_))

rw-sep-body

forall([mrt_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-sep-body(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) iff forsome([mrg_, mra_, mrb_, mrc_], mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-sep(mrc_, mra_), enc-sep(mrc_, mrb_)] and enc-sep(mrc_, mra_) in clt and enc-sep(mrc_, mrb_) in clt and forsome([mrfs_ in set], mrfs_ subset nn and card(mrfs_) in nn and forall([mry_ in nn], not(mry_ in mrfs_) implies [2, mrg_, expr-open(mra_, 0, efvar(mry_)), expr-open(mrb_, 0, efvar(mry_))] in mrs_))))

rw-tapp

forall([mrt_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-tapp(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) iff forsome([mrg_, mrh_, mra_, mrb_, mri_], mrx_ = [1, mrg_, eapp(mrh_, mra_), eapp(mrh_, mrb_)] and mrh_ in nn and 11 <= mrh_ and eapp(mrh_, mra_) in clt and eapp(mrh_, mrb_) in clt and mra_ in tuples(expr-set) and mrb_ in tuples(expr-set) and length(mrb_) = length(mra_) and mri_ in nn and 1 <= mri_ and mri_ <= length(mra_) and forall([mrj_ in nn], 1 <= mrj_ implies mrj_ <= length(mra_) implies not(mrj_ = mri_) implies nth(mrj_, mrb_) = nth(mrj_, mra_)) and [1, mrg_, nth(mri_, mra_), nth(mri_, mrb_)] in mrs_))

rw-tbind-body

forall([mrt_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-tbind-body(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) iff forsome([mrg_, mrh_, mra_, mrb_, mrc_], mrx_ = [1, mrg_, ebind(mrh_, 1, [mrc_], mra_), ebind(mrh_, 1, [mrc_], mrb_)] and (mrh_ = 24 or mrh_ = 26) and ebind(mrh_, 1, [mrc_], mra_) in clt and ebind(mrh_, 1, [mrc_], mrb_) in clt and forsome([mrfs_ in set], mrfs_ subset nn and card(mrfs_) in nn and forall([mry_ in nn], not(mry_ in mrfs_) implies [1, mrg_, expr-open(mra_, 0, efvar(mry_)), expr-open(mrb_, 0, efvar(mry_))] in mrs_))))

rw-top

forall([mrt_, mrs_, mrx_], rw-top(mrt_, mrs_, mrx_) iff mrx_ in mrt_)

same-strand-before

forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssm_], same-strand-before(ssp_, ssn_, ssm_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and ssm_ in nodes(ssp_) and nth(2, ssn_) = nth(2, ssm_) and nth(1, ssn_) < nth(1, ssm_))

same-strand-upto

forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssm_], same-strand-upto(ssp_, ssn_, ssm_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and ssm_ in nodes(ssp_) and nth(2, ssn_) = nth(2, ssm_) and nth(1, ssn_) <= nth(1, ssm_))

sends-to

forall([ssp_, ssn_, ssm_], sends-to(ssp_, ssn_, ssm_) iff ssn_ in nodes(ssp_) and ssm_ in nodes(ssp_) and is-sent(ssp_, ssn_) and is-received(ssp_, ssm_) and node-content(ssp_, ssn_) = node-content(ssp_, ssm_))

seq-compact

forall([s], seq-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and forsome([l in pts(s)], converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), l)))))

series-converges

forall([f], series-converges(f) iff converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k))))

series-converges-to

forall([f, l], series-converges-to(f, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)), l))

smith-staircase

forall([a, m, n, d, k], smith-staircase(a, m, n, d, k) iff is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) and k <= m and k <= n and forall([i_ in interval(1, k)], not(entry(d, i_, i_) = zero(a))) and forall([i_ in interval(1, m)], not(i_ <= k) implies forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(d, i_, j_) = zero(a))))

spans

forall([md, n, u, sm], spans(md, n, u, sm) iff forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(u, j_, 1) in sm) and forall([x_ in sm], forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = lincomb(md, n, c_, u))))

strict-atom

forall([md7af_, md7at_], strict-atom(md7af_, md7at_) iff forsome([md7ax_, md7ay_], (md7af_ = enc-in(md7ax_, md7ay_) or md7af_ = enc-eq(md7ax_, md7ay_)) and (md7at_ in strict-reach(md7ax_) or md7at_ in strict-reach(md7ay_))) or forsome([md7pl_, md7pk_, md7pi_], md7af_ = eapp(10, md7pl_) and nth(1, md7pl_) = econst(md7pk_) and pred-code(md7pk_) and md7pi_ in nn and 2 <= md7pi_ and md7pi_ <= length(md7pl_) and md7at_ in strict-reach(nth(md7pi_, md7pl_))))

strict-closed

forall([md7x_], strict-closed(md7x_) iff forall([md7p_, md7q_], md7p_ in md7x_ implies strict-step(md7p_, md7q_) implies md7q_ in md7x_))

strict-head

forall([mchd_], strict-head(mchd_) iff 7 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 8 or 11 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 19 or 30 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 31)

strict-step

forall([md7s_, md7t_], strict-step(md7s_, md7t_) iff forsome([md7h_, md7l_, md7i_], md7s_ = eapp(md7h_, md7l_) and strict-head(md7h_) and md7i_ in nn and 1 <= md7i_ and md7i_ <= length(md7l_) and md7t_ = nth(md7i_, md7l_)) or forsome([md7k_, md7a_, md7b_], (md7k_ = 22 or md7k_ = 24 or md7k_ = 26) and md7s_ = ebind(md7k_, 1, [md7a_], md7b_) and md7t_ = md7a_))

strictly-mono-nn

forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) iff phi in fun(nn, nn) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m < n_ implies phi(m) < phi(n_)))

subsqn

forall([y, f], subsqn(y, f) iff forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))

summable-weight

forall([w], summable-weight(w) iff w in fun(nn, rr) and forall([n in nn], 0 < w(n)) and series-converges(w))

sums-to

forall([grp, f, r], sums-to(grp, f, r) iff forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([fin in set], card(fin) in nn and fin subset dom(f) and forall([ext], ext in set and card(ext) in nn and fin subset ext and ext subset dom(f) implies (nrm(grp))((opr(grp))(r, (inv(grp))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, ext)))) < eps))))

taylor-differentiable

forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, nth-deriv(f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at(nth-deriv(f, k), t, (nth-deriv(f, succ(k)))(t)))))

taylor-differentiable-v

forall([m, f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(k)))(t)))))

top-converges-to

forall([t, f, l], top-converges-to(t, f, l) iff f in fun(nn, pts(t)) and l in pts(t) and forall([ut_ in opens(t)], l in ut_ implies forsome([nt_ in nn], forall([mt_ in nn], nt_ <= mt_ implies f(mt_) in ut_))))

total-head

forall([mchd_], total-head(mchd_) iff 11 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 14 or mchd_ = 19 or 30 <= mchd_ and mchd_ <= 31)

totally-bounded

forall([s], totally-bounded(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], card(f) in nn and is-r-net(s, f, pts(s), r))))

true-closed

forall([md7v_], true-closed(md7v_) iff forall([md7m_, md7n_], md7m_ in md7v_ implies true-step(md7m_, md7n_) implies md7n_ in md7v_))

true-step

forall([md7f_, md7g_], true-step(md7f_, md7g_) iff forsome([md7c_, md7d_], md7f_ = enc-and(md7c_, md7d_) and (md7g_ = md7c_ or md7g_ = md7d_)) or forsome([md7y_, md7o_, md7r_], md7f_ = enc-in(md7y_, enc-sep(md7o_, md7r_)) and md7g_ = expr-open(md7r_, 0, md7y_)))

unif-on-compacta

forall([u, fam, f], unif-on-compacta(u, fam, f) iff forall([wcq_], wcq_ subset u implies is-compact(subspace-ms(cc-ms, wcq_)) implies forall([wce_], pos-rr(wce_) implies forsome([wcn_ in nn], forall([wck_ in nn], wcn_ <= wck_ implies forall([wcy_ in wcq_], magnitude((fam(wck_))(wcy_) - f(wcy_)) <= wce_))))))

uniquely-originates

forall([ssp_, ssu_], uniquely-originates(ssp_, ssu_) iff forsome([ssn_], originates(ssp_, ssu_, ssn_) and forall([ssm_], originates(ssp_, ssu_, ssm_) implies ssm_ = ssn_)))

wf-fml

forall([mce_], wf-fml(mce_) iff 2 in expr-wks(mce_))

wf-term

forall([mce_], wf-term(mce_) iff 1 in expr-wks(mce_))

zeros-accumulate-at

forall([u, f, a], zeros-accumulate-at(u, f, a) iff forall([acr_], pos-rr(acr_) implies forsome([acx_ in u], acx_ in ball(cc-ms, a, acr_) and not(acx_ = a) and f(acx_) = 0)))

zz-coprime

forall([a, b], zz-coprime(a, b) iff zz-is-gcd(a, b, 1))

zz-divides

forall([a, b], zz-divides(a, b) iff a in zz and b in zz and forsome([c_ in zz], b = a * c_))

zz-is-gcd

forall([a, b, d], zz-is-gcd(a, b, d) iff zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b) and forall([e_ in zz], zz-divides(e_, a) implies zz-divides(e_, b) implies zz-divides(e_, d)))

zz-is-gcd-set

forall([s, d], zz-is-gcd-set(s, d) iff d in zz and forall([gsx_ in s], zz-divides(d, gsx_)) and forall([gse_ in zz], forall([gsx_ in s], zz-divides(gse_, gsx_)) implies zz-divides(gse_, d)))

Recursively-defined functoids

Defined by primitive recursion on NN (def-by-nn-recursion): a name-zero base and a name-succ step. The extra parameters range over a class (a ring, a monoid, ...), so each is a functoid, not a function.

comb-kk

forall([r, x, y], comb-kk(r, x, y, 0) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, if(k = 0, one(r), zero(r))))

forall([r, x, y, m in nn], comb-kk(r, x, y, succ(m)) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k)))))

dc-iter

forall([s, a, nxt], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, 0) == a)

forall([s, a, nxt, n in nn], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, succ(n)) == choice({y_ in s: y_ in nxt(n, dc-iter(s, a, nxt, n))}))

det

forall([r, a], det(r, 0, a) == one(r))

forall([r, n, a], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) == finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), succ(j)), (mul(r))(entry(a, 1, j), det(r, n, minor(a, 1, j, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))

enc-ande

forall([mcl_, mca_], enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, 0) == enc-truth)

forall([mcl_, mca_, mcn_ in nn], enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, mcn_), enc-in(nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_), mca_)))

enc-andl

forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-truth)

forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(mcx_, nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

enc-cartl

forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-truth)

forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(enc-nth(enc-num(succ(mcn_)), mcx_), nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

enc-cch

forall([mix_, mia_, min_], enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, 0) == enc-eq(mix_, enc-list(idx-list(min_))))

forall([mix_, mia_, min_, mik_ in nn], enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, succ(mik_)) == enc-ex(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), nth(min_ - mik_, mia_)), enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, mik_))))

enc-eql

forall([mcl_, mca_], enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, 0) == enc-truth)

forall([mcl_, mca_, mcn_ in nn], enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, mcn_), enc-eq(nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_), nth(succ(mcn_), mca_))))

enc-orl

forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-falsity)

forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-or(enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(mcx_, nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

enc-tcj

forall([mia_, mib_, min_], enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, 0) == enc-truth)

forall([mia_, mib_, min_, mik_ in nn], enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, succ(mik_)) == if(mik_ = 0, enc-eq(nth(min_, mia_), nth(min_, mib_)), enc-and(enc-eq(nth(min_ - mik_, mia_), nth(min_ - mik_, mib_)), enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, mik_))))

expr-stage

expr-stage(0) == union(cartesian(pair(0, 0), nn), union(cartesian(pair(1, 1), nn), cartesian(pair(2, 2), nn)))

forall([mxn_ in nn], expr-stage(succ(mxn_)) == union(expr-stage(mxn_), union(cartesian(pair(3, 3), nn, tuples(expr-stage(mxn_))), cartesian(pair(4, 4), nn, nn, tuples(expr-stage(mxn_)), expr-stage(mxn_)))))

factorial

factorial(0) = succ(0)

forall([k in nn], factorial(succ(k)) = succ(k) * factorial(k))

falling

forall([n], falling(n, 0) == succ(0))

forall([n, k in nn], falling(n, succ(k)) == (n - k) * falling(n, k))

fv-stage

fv-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 0, pair(nth(2, mxe_), nth(2, mxe_)), empty-set))

forall([mxn_ in nn], fv-stage(succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (fv-stage(mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, big-union(mxw_, make-set(nth(3, mxe_)), (fv-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)), union(big-union(mxw_, make-set(nth(4, mxe_)), (fv-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)), (fv-stage(mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_)))))))

lcd-stage

lcd-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 1, {mxd_ in nn: nth(2, mxe_) < mxd_}, nn))

forall([mxn_ in nn], lcd-stage(succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(3, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_))}, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(4, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)) and mxd_ + nth(3, mxe_) in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))}))))

mpow

forall([m, x], mpow(m, x, 0) == iden(m))

forall([m, x, n in nn], mpow(m, x, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(x, mpow(m, x, n)))

msg-stage

forall([smt_, smk_], msg-stage(smt_, smk_, 0) == smt_)

forall([smt_, smk_, smn_ in nn], msg-stage(smt_, smk_, succ(smn_)) == union(msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_), union(cartesian(pair(1, 1), tuples(msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_))), cartesian(pair(2, 2), msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_), smk_))))

nth-deriv

forall([f], nth-deriv(f, 0) == f)

forall([f, n in nn], nth-deriv(f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(nth-deriv(f, n), x)))

nth-deriv-on

forall([ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_], nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, 0) == ndvf_)

forall([ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, ndvn_ in nn], nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, succ(ndvn_)) == vnb-lambda(ndvz_, ndvu_, deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, ndvn_), ndvz_)))

nth-deriv-v

forall([m, f], nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0) == f)

forall([m, f, n in nn], nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, n), x)))

open-stage

forall([mxu_], open-stage(mxu_, 0) == vnb-lambda([mxe_, mxk_], cartesian(expr-stage(0), nn), if(mxe_ = [1, mxk_], mxu_, mxe_)))

forall([mxu_, mxn_ in nn], open-stage(mxu_, succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda([mxe_, mxk_], cartesian(expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), nn), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (open-stage(mxu_, mxn_))(mxe_, mxk_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, [3, nth(2, mxe_), maplk(open-stage(mxu_, mxn_), nth(3, mxe_), mxk_)], [4, nth(2, mxe_), nth(3, mxe_), maplk(open-stage(mxu_, mxn_), nth(4, mxe_), mxk_), (open-stage(mxu_, mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_), mxk_ + nth(3, mxe_))]))))

prod-ord

forall([m, f], prod-ord(m, f, 0) == iden(m))

forall([m, f, n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(prod-ord(m, f, n), f(n)))

reduce

forall([op, f], reduce(op, f, 1) == f(0))

forall([op, f, n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies reduce(op, f, succ(n)) == op(reduce(op, f, n), f(n)))

ring-prod-n

forall([f], ring-prod-n(f, 0) == zero-ring)

forall([f, n in nn], ring-prod-n(f, succ(n)) == ring-prod(ring-prod-n(f, n), f(n)))

rn-maxabs

forall([rnmx_], rn-maxabs(rnmx_, 0) == 0)

forall([rnmx_, rnmk_ in nn], rn-maxabs(rnmx_, succ(rnmk_)) == max(rn-maxabs(rnmx_, rnmk_), abs(rnmx_(succ(rnmk_)))))

subst-stage

forall([mxm_, mxu_], subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, 0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(mxe_ = [0, mxm_], mxu_, mxe_)))

forall([mxm_, mxu_, mxn_ in nn], subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, [3, nth(2, mxe_), mapl(subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_), nth(3, mxe_))], [4, nth(2, mxe_), nth(3, mxe_), mapl(subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_), nth(4, mxe_)), (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))]))))

sum

forall([r, f], sum(r, f, 0) == zero(r))

forall([r, f, n in nn], sum(r, f, succ(n)) == (add(r))(sum(r, f, n), f(n)))

sum-ag

forall([ag, f], sum-ag(ag, f, 0) == iden(ag))

forall([ag, f, n in nn], sum-ag(ag, f, succ(n)) == (opr(ag))(sum-ag(ag, f, n), f(n)))

taylor-poly-v

forall([m, f, a, x], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, 0) == (act(m))((x - a) ^ 0 * recip(factorial(0)), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0))(a)))

forall([m, f, a, x, n in nn], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, succ(n)) == (vadd(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n), (act(m))((x - a) ^ succ(n) * recip(factorial(succ(n))), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(a))))

trinum

trinum(0) == 0

forall([n_ in nn], trinum(succ(n_)) == trinum(n_) + succ(n_))

wks-stage

wks-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mce_, expr-stage(0), pair(1, 1))

forall([mcn_ in nn], wks-stage(succ(mcn_)) == vnb-lambda(mce_, expr-stage(succ(mcn_)), if(mce_ in expr-stage(mcn_), (wks-stage(mcn_))(mce_), if(nth(1, mce_) = 3, {mckk_ in nn: mckk_ = hd-res(nth(2, mce_)) and app-ar-ok(nth(2, mce_), length(nth(3, mce_))) and forall([mcw_ in make-set(nth(3, mce_))], hd-argk(nth(2, mce_)) in (wks-stage(mcn_))(mcw_))}, {mckk_ in nn: mckk_ = hd-bres(nth(2, mce_)) and bind-sig(nth(2, mce_), nth(3, mce_), length(nth(4, mce_))) and forall([mcw_ in make-set(nth(4, mce_))], 1 in (wks-stage(mcn_))(mcw_)) and hd-body(nth(2, mce_)) in (wks-stage(mcn_))(nth(5, mce_))}))))

zen

forall([x], zen(x, 0) = choice(x))

forall([x, alpha in ord], zen(x, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-le(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))

forall([x, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zen(x, lam) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))

zkept

forall([phi, grd, porel], zkept(phi, grd, porel, 0) = empty-set)

forall([phi, grd, porel, alpha in ord], zkept(phi, grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = union(zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), keep-set(phi, grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), alpha)))

forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, lam) = big-union(beta, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta)))

zup

forall([grd, porel], zup(grd, porel, 0) = choice(grd))

forall([grd, porel, alpha in ord], zup(grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in grd: is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, alpha), y_)}))

forall([grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zup(grd, porel, lam) = choice({y_ in grd: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies [zup(grd, porel, c_), y_] in porel)}))

Constants & other defined terms

def-constant terms fixed by characterizing axioms.

ax-fun-app

ax-nn-ind

cc-hat-top

lft-group

lfts

nn-minus

pi

qq-field

Unfold-only constructors (functoids)

def-functoid heads, written name(params) := body: the macete rewrites name(args) to that body, and there is no theorem — which is why these are absent from the def-* registry above. FUNCTORS.md lists the same set split into set/structure-valued and value functoids.

TRAP worth knowing before citing one: mac unfolds a functoid in a GOAL, but mac-h cannot unfold it in an assumption — def-functoid installs no theorem for it to look up, so mac-h warns unknown theorem/macete and the driver continues with the hypothesis untouched. A constructor whose members get read out of the CONTEXT therefore also carries a membership iff, stamped definitional, in the section below — that iff is a consequence of the definition here, not the definition.

abs-fn

abs-fn(omega, f) := vnb-lambda(afx_, omega, abs(f(afx_)))

adj

adj(r, n, a) := matof(succ(n), succ(n), vnb-lambda([adu_, adv_], cartesian(interval(1, succ(n)), interval(1, succ(n))), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), adu_ + adv_), det(r, n, minor(a, adv_, adu_, n)))))

ae-rel

ae-rel(omega, ca, mu, sf) := {aep_ in cartesian(sf, sf): forsome([aen_], is-null-set(omega, ca, mu, aen_) and forall([aex_ in omega], not(aex_ in aen_) implies (nth(1, aep_))(aex_) = (nth(2, aep_))(aex_)))}

ae-setoid

ae-setoid(omega, ca, mu, sf) := [sf, ae-rel(omega, ca, mu, sf)]

ball

ball(s, c, r) := {y in pts(s): (dist(s))(c, y) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, y) = r)}

ball-cover(s, r) := image(vnb-lambda(c, pts(s), ball(s, c, r)), pts(s))

bdd-metric

bdd-metric(s) := [pts(s), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), (/)((dist(s))(u, v), 1 + (dist(s))(u, v)))]

bernstein-basis

bernstein-basis(n, x) := comb-kk(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), x, 1 - x, n)

bernstein-poly

bernstein-poly(f, n, x) := series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(l_, nn, f((/)(l_, n)) * (bernstein-basis(n, x))(l_)), succ(n))

block

block(p, k, l) := matof(k, l, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, l)), entry(p, i, j)))

border

borel-sets

c-metric-w

cact

cseq(m) := {f in fun(nn, pts(m)): is-cauchy-seq(m, f)}

csip-cips

csip-cips(v) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field), vec(v), vadd(v), vzero(v), vneg(v), cact(v), crip(v)]

csip-qj

csip-qj(v) := descend(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v)), vnb-lambda(sqa_, vec(v), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v)), (jop(v))(sqa_))))

csip-quotient

csip-quotient(v) := [scal(v), quotient(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v))), sip-qvadd(csip-as-sip(v)), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(v)), vzero(v)), sip-qvneg(csip-as-sip(v)), sip-qact(csip-as-sip(v)), sip-qrip(csip-as-sip(v)), csip-qj(v)]

ctx-add

ctx-add(mcg_, mca_) := union(mcg_, pair(mca_, mca_))

ctx-fv

ctx-fv(mcg_) := big-union(mcw_, mcg_, expr-fv(mcw_))

cx-act

cx-act(v) := vnb-lambda([cr_, cp_], cartesian(carr(scal(v)), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v))), [(act(v))(cr_, nth(1, cp_)), (act(v))(cr_, nth(2, cp_))])

cx-j

cx-j(v) := vnb-lambda(cp_, cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), [(vneg(v))(nth(2, cp_)), nth(1, cp_)])

cx-rip

cx-rip(v) := vnb-lambda([cp_, cq_], cartesian(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v))), (rip(v))(nth(1, cp_), nth(1, cq_)) + (rip(v))(nth(2, cp_), nth(2, cq_)))

cx-sip

cx-sip(v) := [scal(v), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cx-vadd(v), [vzero(v), vzero(v)], cx-vneg(v), cx-act(v), cx-rip(v)]

cx-vadd

cx-vadd(v) := vnb-lambda([cp_, cq_], cartesian(cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), cartesian(vec(v), vec(v))), [(vadd(v))(nth(1, cp_), nth(1, cq_)), (vadd(v))(nth(2, cp_), nth(2, cq_))])

cx-vneg

cx-vneg(v) := vnb-lambda(cp_, cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), [(vneg(v))(nth(1, cp_)), (vneg(v))(nth(2, cp_))])

deg

deg(a, p) := iota(n_, n_ in nn and deg-bound(a, p, n_) and forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a, p, j_) implies n_ <= j_))

deriv

deriv-v

deriv-v(m, f, a) := iota(l, is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l))

derivable

descend

descend(s, f) := vnb-lambda(c, quotient(s), iota(z, forsome([a in c], z = f(a))))

descend2

difference

dual

dual(m) := [rr-normed-field, dual-vec(m), vnb-lambda([f_, g_], cartesian(dual-vec(m), dual-vec(m)), vnb-lambda(x_, vec(m), f_(x_) + g_(x_))), vnb-lambda(x_, vec(m), 0), vnb-lambda(f_, dual-vec(m), vnb-lambda(x_, vec(m), -f_(x_))), vnb-lambda([r_, f_], cartesian(rr, dual-vec(m)), vnb-lambda(x_, vec(m), r_ * f_(x_))), vnb-lambda(f_, dual-vec(m), dual-norm(m, f_))]

dual-norm

dual-norm(m, f) := iota(c_, c_ in rr and 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) and forall([d_], d_ in rr and 0 <= d_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= d_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies c_ <= d_))

dual-norm-on

dual-norm-on(m, s, f) := iota(c_, c_ in rr and 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) and forall([d_], d_ in rr and 0 <= d_ and forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= d_ * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies c_ <= d_))

dual-vec

dual-vec(m) := {f_ in fun(vec(m), rr): is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f_)}

dynkin-generated

dynkin-generated(omega, ce) := {dga_ in power(omega): forall([dgb_], is-dynkin-system(omega, dgb_) and ce subset dgb_ implies dga_ in dgb_)}

elem-f

elem-f(a, n, k, l) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = k and j = l or i = l and j = k or i = j and not(i = k) and not(i = l), one(a), zero(a))))

elem-g

elem-g(a, n, r, k, l) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, one(a), if(i = k and j = l, r, zero(a)))))

elem-gen

elem-gen(r, n) := intersection-of({egs_ in power(mat(n, n, carr(r))): identmat(r, n) in egs_ and forall([egx_ in egs_, ege_], is-elementary(r, n, ege_) implies matmul(r, egx_, ege_) in egs_)})

elem-h

elem-h(a, n, r, k) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, if(i = k, r, one(a)), zero(a))))
