ip-normed-ag(v) := [vec(v), vadd(v), vzero(v), vneg(v), vnb-lambda(x_, vec(v), ip-norm(v, x_))]

j-cart

j-cart(mit_) := {mix_ in rw-univ: forsome([mig_, miz_, mia_], mix_ = [2, mig_, enc-in(miz_, enc-cart(mia_)), enc-cch(miz_, mia_, length(mia_), length(mia_))] and mig_ in ctx-set and 1 <= length(mia_))}

j-lb

j-lb(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mra_, mrb_, mre_], mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-apply(enc-lam(mra_, mrb_), mre_), expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_)] and enc-apply(enc-lam(mra_, mrb_), mre_) in clt and expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_) in clt and [mrg_, enc-in(mre_, mra_)] in derivable(mrt_))}

j-len

j-len(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrl_], mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-length(enc-list(mrl_)), enc-num(length(mrl_))] and enc-length(enc-list(mrl_)) in clt and enc-num(length(mrl_)) in clt and [mrg_, enc-eq(enc-list(mrl_), enc-list(mrl_))] in derivable(mrt_))}

j-mac

j-mac(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrc_, mra_, mrb_, mre_ in clt], [mrg_, expr-open(mrc_, 0, mre_)] in derivable(mrt_) and [mrg_, enc-eq(mre_, mre_)] in derivable(mrt_) and (mrx_ = [1, mrg_, expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_), expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_)] and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-qeq(mra_, mrb_))) in mrt_ and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-qeq(mra_, mrb_))) in clf and expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_) in clt and expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_) in clt or mrx_ = [2, mrg_, expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_), expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_)] and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-iff(mra_, mrb_))) in mrt_ and enc-all(enc-imp(mrc_, enc-iff(mra_, mrb_))) in clf and expr-open(mra_, 0, mre_) in clf and expr-open(mrb_, 0, mre_) in clf))}

j-nth

j-nth(mrt_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forsome([mrg_, mrl_, mrk_], mrx_ = [1, mrg_, enc-nth(enc-num(mrk_), enc-list(mrl_)), nth(mrk_, mrl_)] and mrk_ in nn and 1 <= mrk_ and mrk_ <= length(mrl_) and enc-nth(enc-num(mrk_), enc-list(mrl_)) in clt and nth(mrk_, mrl_) in clt and [mrg_, enc-eq(enc-list(mrl_), enc-list(mrl_))] in derivable(mrt_))}

j-teq

j-teq(mit_) := {mix_ in rw-univ: forsome([mig_, mia_, mib_], mix_ = [2, mig_, enc-eq(enc-list(mia_), enc-list(mib_)), enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, length(mia_), length(mia_))] and mig_ in ctx-set and length(mia_) = length(mib_))}

juxta

juxta(jxp_, jxq_, jxa_, jxb_, jxc_) := vnb-lambda(jxt_, ccint(jxa_, jxc_), if(jxt_ <= jxb_, jxp_(jxt_), jxq_(jxt_)))

keep-set

keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha) := {y in grd: y = phi(alpha) and forall([z in kset], [z, y] in porel)}

l2-act

l2-act(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda([lr_, lf_], cartesian(rr, l2-set(omega, ca, mu)), vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lr_ * lf_(lx_)))

l2-rip

l2-rip(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda([lf_, lg_], cartesian(l2-set(omega, ca, mu), l2-set(omega, ca, mu)), rintegral(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lf_(lx_) * lg_(lx_))))

l2-set

l2-set(omega, ca, mu) := {lf_ in fun(omega, rr): is-real-measurable-fn(omega, ca, lf_) and is-integrable(omega, ca, mu, vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lf_(lx_) * lf_(lx_)))}

l2-space

l2-space(omega, ca, mu) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), l2-set(omega, ca, mu), l2-vadd(omega, ca, mu), l2-vzero(omega), l2-vneg(omega, ca, mu), l2-act(omega, ca, mu), l2-rip(omega, ca, mu)]

l2-vadd

l2-vadd(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda([lf_, lg_], cartesian(l2-set(omega, ca, mu), l2-set(omega, ca, mu)), vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, lf_(lx_) + lg_(lx_)))

l2-vneg

l2-vneg(omega, ca, mu) := vnb-lambda(lf_, l2-set(omega, ca, mu), vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, -lf_(lx_)))

l2-vzero

l2-vzero(omega) := vnb-lambda(lx_, omega, 0)

lastcoeff-set

lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm) := {r_ in carr(scal(md)): forsome([c_ in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c_, 1, succ(p)) = r_ and lincomb(md, succ(p), c_, u) in sm)}

leadcoef

leadcoef(a, p) := p(deg(a, p))

lft

lft(a, b, c, d) := vnb-lambda(lfz_, cc-hat, if(lfz_ = cc-inf, if(c = 0, cc-inf, a * recip(c)), if(c * lfz_ + d = 0, cc-inf, (a * lfz_ + b) * recip(c * lfz_ + d))))

lincomb

lincomb(md, n, c, u) := finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))), interval(1, n))

line

line(m, v) := {y_ in vec(m): forsome([r_ in rr], y_ = (act(m))(r_, v))}

line-int

line-int(pf, pgam, dgam, a, b) := cc-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), pf(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)), a, b)

log

log(x_) := c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), 1, x_)

lp-norm

lp-norm(omega, ca, mu, lpp_, lpf_) := ep-norm(omega, ca, mu, lpp_, abs-fn(omega, lpf_))

lst

lst(sml_) := [1, sml_]

mapl

mapl(mxf_, mxl_) := iota(mxr_, mxr_ in tuples(set) and length(mxr_) = length(mxl_) and forall([mxi_ in nn], 1 <= mxi_ implies mxi_ <= length(mxl_) implies nth(mxi_, mxr_) = mxf_(nth(mxi_, mxl_))))

maplk

maplk(mxf_, mxl_, mxk_) := iota(mxr_, mxr_ in tuples(set) and length(mxr_) = length(mxl_) and forall([mxi_ in nn], 1 <= mxi_ implies mxi_ <= length(mxl_) implies nth(mxi_, mxr_) = mxf_(nth(mxi_, mxl_), mxk_)))

mat

mat(m, n, x) := {p in matrix(x): n in nn and (length(p) = 0 implies m = 0) and (not(length(p) = 0) implies size(p) = [m, n])}

mat-lft

mat-lft(s) := lft(entry(s, 1, 1), entry(s, 1, 2), entry(s, 2, 1), entry(s, 2, 2))

mat-ring

mat-ring(a, n) := [mat(n, n, carr(a)), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matadd(a, p, q)), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matmul(a, p, q)), vnb-lambda(p, mat(n, n, carr(a)), matneg(a, p)), zeromat(a, n, n), identmat(a, n)]

matact

matact(md, p, u) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(u)), vnb-lambda([i, c], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(u)))), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, nth(2, size(p))), (act(md))(entry(p, i, j), entry(u, j, c))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p))))))

matadd

matadd(a, p, q) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(p)), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p)))), (add(a))(entry(p, i, j), entry(q, i, j))))

matmul

matmul(a, p, q) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(q)), vnb-lambda([i, k], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(q)))), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, nth(2, size(p))), (mul(a))(entry(p, i, j), entry(q, j, k))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p))))))

matneg

matneg(a, p) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(p)), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p)))), (neg(a))(entry(p, i, j))))

matof

matof(m, n, g) := iota(p, p in mat(m, n, image(g, cartesian(interval(1, m), interval(1, n)))) and forall([i in interval(1, m), j in interval(1, n)], entry(p, i, j) = g(i, j)))

matscale

matscale(a, r, p) := matof(nth(1, size(p)), nth(2, size(p)), vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, nth(1, size(p))), interval(1, nth(2, size(p)))), (mul(a))(r, entry(p, i, j))))

matunit

matunit(a, n, k, l) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = k and j = l, one(a), zero(a))))

method-i-outer

method-i-outer(omega, cls, pm) := vnb-lambda(cve_, power(omega), einf(cover-sums(omega, cls, pm, cve_)))

metric-top

metric-top(md) := [pts(md), {u in power(pts(md)): is-open(md, u)}]

minor

minor(s, r, c, n) := matof(n, n, vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), entry(s, if(i < r, i, succ(i)), if(j < c, j, succ(j)))))

monalg

monalg(a, m) := [finsupp(a, m), vnb-lambda([f, g], cartesian(finsupp(a, m), finsupp(a, m)), monalg-add(a, m, f, g)), vnb-lambda([f, g], cartesian(finsupp(a, m), finsupp(a, m)), monalg-mul(a, m, f, g)), vnb-lambda(f, finsupp(a, m), monalg-neg(a, m, f)), monalg-zero(a, m), monalg-one(a, m)]

monalg-add

monalg-add(a, m, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), (add(a))(f(x_), g(x_)))

monalg-mul

monalg-mul(a, m, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a, m, f), supp(a, m, g)): (opr(m))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a))(f(nth(1, p)), g(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a, m, f), supp(a, m, g)): (opr(m))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}))

monalg-neg

monalg-neg(a, m, f) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), (neg(a))(f(x_)))

monalg-one

monalg-one(a, m) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), if(x_ = iden(m), one(a), zero(a)))

monalg-zero

monalg-zero(a, m) := vnb-lambda(x_, carr(m), zero(a))

monomial

monomial(a, c, n) := vnb-lambda(x_, nn, if(x_ = n, c, zero(a)))

monotone-class-generated

monotone-class-generated(omega, ce) := {mca_ in power(omega): forall([mcb_], is-monotone-class(omega, mcb_) and ce subset mcb_ implies mca_ in mcb_)}

msg-rank

msg-rank(smt_, smk_, smm_) := iota(smr_, smr_ in nn and smm_ in msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smr_) and forall([smj_ in nn], smm_ in msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smj_) implies smr_ <= smj_))

msg-set

msg-set(smt_, smk_) := big-union(smn_, nn, msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_))

nag-metric-space

nag-metric-space(nag) := [carr(nag), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(carr(nag), carr(nag)), (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v))))]

neg-part

neg-part(omega, f) := vnb-lambda(npx_, omega, if(0 <= f(npx_), 0, -f(npx_)))

nf-metric-space

nf-metric-space(nf) := [carr(nf), vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(carr(nf), carr(nf)), (fnrm(nf))((add(nf))(x, (neg(nf))(y))))]

nn-enum

nn-enum(s) := choice({f in fun(nn, s): forall([m in nn, n in nn], m < n implies f(m) < f(n))})

nnfst

nnfst(n_) := iota(a_, a_ in nn and forsome([b_ in nn], nnpair(a_, b_) = n_))

nnpair

nnpair(i_, j_) := trinum(i_ + j_) + j_

nnsnd

nnsnd(n_) := iota(b_, b_ in nn and forsome([a_ in nn], nnpair(a_, b_) = n_))

node-content

node-content(ssp_, ssn_) := nth(2, nth(nth(1, ssn_), (strand-map(ssp_))(nth(2, ssn_))))

node-dir

node-dir(ssp_, ssn_) := nth(1, nth(nth(1, ssn_), (strand-map(ssp_))(nth(2, ssn_))))

nodes

nodes(ssp_) := {ssn_ in cartesian(nn, ids(ssp_)): 1 <= nth(1, ssn_) and nth(1, ssn_) <= length((strand-map(ssp_))(nth(2, ssn_)))}

nvs-metric-space

nvs-metric-space(m) := [vec(m), vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(vec(m), vec(m)), (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y))))]

ocint

ocint(a, b) := {osq_ in rr: a < osq_ and osq_ <= b}

ooint

ooint(a, b) := {icx_ in rr: a < icx_ and icx_ < b}

open-fun

open-fun(mxu_) := vnb-lambda([mxw_, mxj_], cartesian(expr-set, nn), expr-open(mxw_, mxj_, mxu_))

permutations

permutations(n) := injection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n))

perp

perp(v, ks) := {prp_ in vec(v): forall([prk_ in ks], (ip(v))(prp_, prk_) = 0)}

poly

poly(a) := monalg(a, nn-add-monoid)

pos-part

pos-part(omega, f) := vnb-lambda(ppx_, omega, if(0 <= f(ppx_), f(ppx_), 0))

prec-strict

prec-strict(spc_, snd_, ssd_) := {ssp_ in cartesian(snd_, snd_): forall([ssq_ in power(cartesian(snd_, snd_))], is-prec-closed(spc_, snd_, ssd_, ssq_) implies ssp_ in ssq_)}

precedes

precedes(ssc_) := union(precedes-strict(ssc_), {ssp_ in cartesian(bnodes(ssc_), bnodes(ssc_)): nth(1, ssp_) = nth(2, ssp_)})

precedes-strict

precedes-strict(ssc_) := prec-strict(bspace(ssc_), bnodes(ssc_), bsend(ssc_))

pred

pred(n_) := iota(m_, m_ in nn and succ(m_) = n_)

preimage

preimage(s, f, v) := {a in pts(s): f(a) in v}

principal-ideal

principal-ideal(s, a) := {x in carr(s): forsome([r in carr(s)], x = (mul(s))(r, a))}

prod-ring

prod-ring(r, f, s) := finprod(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(r), f, s)

prod-set

prod-set(cm, s, f) := finsum(cm, f, s)

product-carrier

product-carrier(ms) := {x in fun(nn, big-union(n, nn, pts(ms(n)))): forall([n in nn], x(n) in pts(ms(n)))}

product-measure

product-measure(xs, ca, mu, ys, cb, nu) := vnb-lambda(pmc_, product-sigma(xs, ca, ys, cb), integral(xs, ca, mu, x-section-measure(xs, ys, nu, pmc_)))

product-metric

product-metric(ms) := product-metric-w(ms, vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))

product-metric-w

product-metric-w(ms, w) := [product-carrier(ms), vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(product-carrier(ms), product-carrier(ms)), iota(l, series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))), l)))]

product-proj

product-proj(ms, n) := vnb-lambda(x, product-carrier(ms), x(n))

product-sigma

product-sigma(xs, ca, ys, cb) := sigma-generated(cartesian(xs, ys), rectangles(xs, ca, ys, cb))

proj

proj(s) := vnb-lambda(a, pts(s), class(s, a))

ps-partial-sum

ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k) := sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(n, nn, coef(n) * x ^ n), k)

pseudo-gauge-top

pseudo-gauge-top(fam, ground) := [ground, {u in power(ground): is-open-gauge(fam, ground, u)}]

pt-set-dist

pt-set-dist(m, x, es) := einf({mov_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([moz_ in es], mov_ = (dist(m))(x, moz_))})

ptwise-eplus

ptwise-eplus(omega, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, eplus(f(x_), g(x_)))

ptwise-epow

ptwise-epow(epo_, epf_, epp_) := vnb-lambda(epx_, epo_, epow(epf_(epx_), epp_))

ptwise-etimes

ptwise-etimes(omega, f, g) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, etimes(f(x_), g(x_)))

ptwise-liminf

ptwise-liminf(omega, cf) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, eliminf(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, (cf(n_))(x_))))

ptwise-scale

ptwise-scale(omega, c, f) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, etimes(c, f(x_)))

ptwise-sup

ptwise-sup(omega, cf) := vnb-lambda(x_, omega, esup(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, (cf(n_))(x_)), nn)))

push-forward

push-forward(omega, cb, mu, h) := vnb-lambda(pwb_, cb, mu({pwx_ in omega: h(pwx_) in pwb_}))

pw-int

pw-int(pphi_, a, b) := iota(pav_, pav_ in rr and forsome([paw_], is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b) and pav_ = paw_(b) - paw_(a)))

quotient

quotient(s) := image(proj(s), pts(s))

r-exp

r-exp(y_) := iota(x_, x_ in rr and 0 < x_ and log(x_) = y_)

ran

ran(f) := image(f, dom(f))

recip-star

recip-star(u_) := if(u_ = 0, 0, recip(u_))

rectangles

rectangles(xs, ca, ys, cb) := {rcr_ in power(cartesian(xs, ys)): forsome([rca_ in ca, rcb_ in cb], rcr_ = cartesian(rca_, rcb_))}

related

related(s, a, b) := [a, b] in rel(s)

respects

respects(s, f) := forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], related(s, a, b) implies f(a) = f(b))

respects2

respects2(s, f) := forall([a, b, a_, b_], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a, a_) and related(s, b, b_) implies f(a, b) = f(a_, b_))

restrict

restrict(f, a) := vnb-lambda(rsx_, a, f(rsx_))

rewr

rewr(mrt_, mrk_) := {mrx_ in rw-univ: forall([mrs_ in power(rw-univ)], rw-closed(mrt_, mrk_, mrs_) implies mrx_ in mrs_)}

ring-power

ring-power(r, x, n) := mpow(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(r), x, n)

ring-prod

ring-prod(x, y) := [cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), cartesian(carr(x), carr(y))), [(add(x))(nth(1, p), nth(1, q)), (add(y))(nth(2, p), nth(2, q))]), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), cartesian(carr(x), carr(y))), [(mul(x))(nth(1, p), nth(1, q)), (mul(y))(nth(2, p), nth(2, q))]), vnb-lambda(p, cartesian(carr(x), carr(y)), [(neg(x))(nth(1, p)), (neg(y))(nth(2, p))]), [zero(x), zero(y)], [one(x), one(y)]]

ringoid-quotient

ringoid-quotient(r) := quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))

ringoid-quotient-ring

ringoid-quotient-ring(r) := [quotient(ringoid-setoid(r)), descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))), descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b)))), descend(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a)))), class(ringoid-setoid(r), zero(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), one(r))]

ringoid-rel

ringoid-rel(r) := {p in cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)): (add(r))(nth(1, p), (neg(r))(nth(2, p))) in idl(r)}

ringoid-setoid

ringoid-setoid(r) := [carr(r), ringoid-rel(r)]

rintegral

rintegral(omega, ca, mu, f) := integral(omega, ca, mu, pos-part(omega, f)) - integral(omega, ca, mu, neg-part(omega, f))

rm-ball

rm-ball(s, dseq, n) := closed-ball(s, dseq(nnfst(n)), recip(nnsnd(n) + 1))

rm-code

rm-code(s, dseq, x) := vnb-lambda(rox_, nn, if(x in rm-ball(s, dseq, rox_), 1, 0))

rm-cyl

rm-cyl(s, dseq, a, nb) := {rce_ in rm-k(s, dseq): forall([rci_ in nn], rci_ < nb implies rce_(rci_) = a(rci_))}

rm-k

rm-k(s, dseq) := {rke_ in pts(cantor-ms): forall([rkn_ in nn], forsome([rky_], rm-realizes(s, dseq, rke_, rkn_, rky_)))}

rm-kms

rm-kms(s, dseq) := subspace-ms(cantor-ms, rm-k(s, dseq))

rm-pi

rm-pi(s, dseq) := vnb-lambda(rpe_, rm-k(s, dseq), iota(rpy_, rm-address(s, dseq, rpe_, rpy_)))

rm-shell

rm-shell(s, dseq, n) := {rsy_ in pts(s): recip(nnsnd(n) + 1) <= (dist(s))(dseq(nnfst(n)), rsy_)}

rn

rn(n) := [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), fun(interval(1, n), rr), vnb-lambda([rnax_, rnay_], cartesian(fun(interval(1, n), rr), fun(interval(1, n), rr)), vnb-lambda(rnai_, interval(1, n), rnax_(rnai_) + rnay_(rnai_))), vnb-lambda(rnzi_, interval(1, n), 0), vnb-lambda(rnnx_, fun(interval(1, n), rr), vnb-lambda(rnni_, interval(1, n), -rnnx_(rnni_))), vnb-lambda([rnsr_, rnsx_], cartesian(rr, fun(interval(1, n), rr)), vnb-lambda(rnsi_, interval(1, n), rnsr_ * rnsx_(rnsi_))), vnb-lambda(rnvx_, fun(interval(1, n), rr), rn-maxabs(rnvx_, n))]

rn-trunc

rn-trunc(n, x, k) := vnb-lambda(rntj_, interval(1, n), if(rntj_ <= k, x(rntj_), 0))

rn-unit

rn-unit(n, i) := vnb-lambda(rnuj_, interval(1, n), if(rnuj_ = i, 1, 0))

road-length

road-length(rld_, rla_, rlb_) := pw-int(vnb-lambda(rlt_, ccint(rla_, rlb_), magnitude(rld_(rlt_))), rla_, rlb_)

rpow-star

rpow-star(x_, s_) := if(0 < x_, r-exp(s_ * log(x_)), if(0 < s_, 0, 1))

sdet

sdet(r, m, n, a, s) := {sdx_ in carr(r): forsome([sdp_, sdq_], sdp_ in injection(interval(1, s), interval(1, m)) and sdq_ in injection(interval(1, s), interval(1, n)) and sdx_ = det(r, s, submatrix(a, s, sdp_, sdq_)))}

seg-curve

seg-curve(m, f, a, eta) := vnb-lambda(u_, rr, f((vadd(m))(a, (act(m))(u_, eta))))

seg-deriv

seg-deriv(sgp_, sgq_) := vnb-lambda(sgs_, ccint(0, 1), sgq_ - sgp_)

seg-int

seg-int(sgf_, sgp_, sgq_) := line-int(sgf_, seg-path(sgp_, sgq_), seg-deriv(sgp_, sgq_), 0, 1)

seg-path

seg-path(sgp_, sgq_) := vnb-lambda(sgs_, ccint(0, 1), sgp_ + sgs_ * (sgq_ - sgp_))

seq-limit

seq-limit(f) := if(converges(rr-ms, f), iota(lm_, converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm_)), 0)

series-limit

series-limit(f) := iota(lm_, series-converges-to(f, lm_))

series-partial-sum

series-partial-sum(f, k) := sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f, k)

set-dist

set-dist(m, aa, bb) := einf({mow_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([mox_ in aa, moy_ in bb], mow_ = (dist(m))(mox_, moy_))})

sigma-generated

sigma-generated(omega, ce) := {a_ in power(omega): forall([cb_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, cb_) and ce subset cb_ implies a_ in cb_)}

simple-integral

simple-integral(omega, mu, s) := finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, vnb-lambda(siy_, image(s, omega), etimes(siy_, mu({six_ in omega: s(six_) = siy_}))), image(s, omega))

singleton

singleton(y_) := {y_}

sip-metric-space

sip-normed-ag

sip-normed-ag(v) := [vec(v), vadd(v), vzero(v), vneg(v), vnb-lambda(shn_, vec(v), sqrt((rip(v))(shn_, shn_)))]

sip-null

sip-null(v) := {sqn_ in vec(v): (rip(v))(sqn_, sqn_) = 0}

sip-perp

sip-perp(v, ks) := {spp_ in vec(v): forall([spk_ in ks], (rip(v))(spp_, spk_) = 0)}

sip-qact

sip-qact(v) := vnb-lambda([sqr_, sqc_], cartesian(rr, quotient(sip-setoid(v))), (sip-qscale(v, sqr_))(sqc_))

sip-qrip

sip-qrip(v) := descend2(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda([sqa_, sqb_], cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), (rip(v))(sqa_, sqb_)))

sip-qscale

sip-qscale(v, r) := descend(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda(sqa_, vec(v), class(sip-setoid(v), (act(v))(r, sqa_))))

sip-quotient

sip-quotient(v) := [scal(v), quotient(sip-setoid(v)), sip-qvadd(v), class(sip-setoid(v), vzero(v)), sip-qvneg(v), sip-qact(v), sip-qrip(v)]

sip-qvadd

sip-qvadd(v) := descend2(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda([sqa_, sqb_], cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)), class(sip-setoid(v), (vadd(v))(sqa_, sqb_))))

sip-qvneg

sip-qvneg(v) := descend(sip-setoid(v), vnb-lambda(sqa_, vec(v), class(sip-setoid(v), (vneg(v))(sqa_))))

sip-rel

sip-rel(v) := {sqp_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)): (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(1, sqp_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, sqp_))), (vadd(v))(nth(1, sqp_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, sqp_)))) = 0}

sip-setoid

sip-setoid(v) := [vec(v), sip-rel(v)]

size

size(m) := [length(m), if(length(m) = 0, 0, length(nth(1, m)))]

snoc-col

snoc-col(w, n, x) := matof(succ(n), 1, vnb-lambda([i_, j_], cartesian(interval(1, succ(n)), interval(1, 1)), if(i_ = succ(n), x, entry(w, i_, 1))))

snoc-row

snoc-row(c, n, r) := matof(1, succ(n), vnb-lambda([i_, j_], cartesian(interval(1, 1), interval(1, succ(n))), if(j_ = succ(n), r, entry(c, 1, j_))))

span

span-add-one

sqn

sqrt

strands

strands(sst_, ssk_) := tuples(directed(sst_, ssk_))

strict-reach

subterm-rel

bijection-membership-iff

forall([x, y, phi], phi in bijection(x, y) iff phi in fun(x, y) and forall([a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b) and forall([w in y], forsome([z in x], phi(z) = w)))

cantor-ms == product-metric(vnb-lambda(ctk_, nn, rm-two-ms))

caratheodory-sets-membership

forall([omega, mu, es_], es_ in caratheodory-sets(omega, mu) iff es_ in power(omega) and is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es_))

cayley-def

cayley == lft(1, -1i, 1, 1i)

cc-coords-def

cc-coords == vnb-lambda(zv_, cc, [real-part(zv_), imag-part(zv_)])

cc-hat-def

cc-hat == union(cc, singleton(cc-inf))

cc-hat-opens-def

cc-hat-opens == {uh_ in power(cc-hat): uh_ subset cc and is-open(cc-ms, uh_) or forsome([kh_], kh_ subset cc and is-compact(subspace-ms(cc-ms, kh_)) and uh_ = difference(cc-hat, kh_))}

cc-hat-top-def

cc-hat-top = [cc-hat, cc-hat-opens]

cc-hat-top@opens

opens(cc-hat-top) == cc-hat-opens

cc-hat-top@pts

pts(cc-hat-top) == cc-hat

cc-matrices-def

cc-matrices == {cmq_ in mat(2, 2, rr): forsome([cma_ in rr, cmb_ in rr], cmq_ = cc-matrix-of(cma_, cmb_))}

cc-matrix-rep-def

cc-matrix-rep == vnb-lambda(cmz_, cc, cc-matrix-of(real-part(cmz_), imag-part(cmz_)))

cc-ms-def

cc-ms = [cc, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))]

cc-ms@dist

dist(cc-ms) == vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))

cc-ms@pts

pts(cc-ms) == cc

cc-normed-field-def

cc-normed-field = [cc, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))]

cc-normed-field@add

add(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_)

cc-normed-field@carr

carr(cc-normed-field) == cc

cc-normed-field@fnrm

fnrm(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))

cc-normed-field@mul

mul(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_)

cc-normed-field@neg

neg(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_)

cc-normed-field@one

one(cc-normed-field) == 1

cc-normed-field@zero

zero(cc-normed-field) == 0

cc-of-pair-def

cc-of-pair == vnb-lambda(pv_, cartesian(rr, rr), nth(1, pv_) + nth(2, pv_) * 1i)

cips-ip-add-left

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = (ip(v))(x_, z_) + (ip(v))(y_, z_)))

cips-ip-conj-sym

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(y_, x_) = conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_))))

cips-ip-definite

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(v)))

cips-ip-homog-left

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (ip(v))(x_, y_)))

cips-ip-zero-vector

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(v)))

cips-scal-carrier

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies carr(scal(v)) = cc)

cips-vadd-vzero

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (vadd(v))(vzero(v), x_) = x_))

clf-def

clf == {mce_ in expr-set: wf-fml(mce_) and expr-lc(mce_)}

closed-intervals-def

closed-intervals == {oci_ in power(rr): oci_ = empty-set or forsome([oca_ in rr, ocb_ in rr], oca_ <= ocb_ and oci_ = ccint(oca_, ocb_))}

clt-def

clt == {mce_ in expr-set: wf-term(mce_) and expr-lc(mce_)}

comb-kk-succ

forall([r, x, y, m in nn], comb-kk(r, x, y, succ(m)) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k)))))

comb-kk-zero

forall([r, x, y], comb-kk(r, x, y, 0) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, if(k = 0, one(r), zero(r))))

ctx-def

ctx-set == {mcg_ in power(clf): card(mcg_) in nn}

dc-iter-succ

forall([s, a, nxt, n in nn], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, succ(n)) == choice({y_ in s: y_ in nxt(n, dc-iter(s, a, nxt, n))}))

dc-iter-zero

forall([s, a, nxt], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, 0) == a)

descent-remainder-sip-as-module

forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(r)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(r)), c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(r))), c0 in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(r))), q in carr(scal(r)), b in carr(scal(r))], entry(c0, 1, succ(n)) = b implies entry(c, 1, succ(n)) = (mul(scal(r)))(q, b) implies forsome([y in span(sip-as-module(r), n, block(u, n, 1))], lincomb(sip-as-module(r), succ(n), c, u) = (vadd(r))(y, (act(r))(q, lincomb(sip-as-module(r), succ(n), c0, u))) and y = (vadd(r))(lincomb(sip-as-module(r), succ(n), c, u), (act(r))((neg(scal(r)))(q), lincomb(sip-as-module(r), succ(n), c0, u))))))

descent-remainder-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(r)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(r)), c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(r))), c0 in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(r))), q in carr(scal(r)), b in carr(scal(r))], entry(c0, 1, succ(n)) = b implies entry(c, 1, succ(n)) = (mul(scal(r)))(q, b) implies forsome([y in span(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), n, block(u, n, 1))], lincomb(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), succ(n), c, u) = (vadd(r))(y, (act(r))(q, lincomb(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), succ(n), c0, u))) and y = (vadd(r))(lincomb(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), succ(n), c, u), (act(r))((neg(scal(r)))(q), lincomb(sip-as-module(csip-as-sip(r)), succ(n), c0, u))))))

det-cofactor

forall([r, n, a], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) == finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), succ(j)), (mul(r))(entry(a, 1, j), det(r, n, minor(a, 1, j, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))

det-zero

forall([r, a], det(r, 0, a) == one(r))

dual-carrier

forall([m], vec(dual(m)) == dual-vec(m))

dual-norm-value

forall([m, f in dual-vec(m)], (vnrm(dual(m)))(f) == dual-norm(m, f))

dual-scal

forall([m], scal(dual(m)) == rr-normed-field)

dual-vec-membership

forall([m, f], f in dual-vec(m) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f))

ell-one-def

ell-one = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_))))}

ell-two-def

ell-two = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_)) * magnitude(x_(k_))))}

enc-ande-succ

forall([mcl_, mca_, mcn_ in nn], enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, mcn_), enc-in(nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_), mca_)))

enc-ande-zero

forall([mcl_, mca_], enc-ande(mcl_, mca_, 0) == enc-truth)

enc-andl-succ

forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(mcx_, nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

enc-andl-zero

forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-andl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-truth)

enc-cartl-succ

forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(enc-nth(enc-num(succ(mcn_)), mcx_), nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

enc-cartl-zero

forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-cartl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-truth)

enc-cch-succ

forall([mix_, mia_, min_, mik_ in nn], enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, succ(mik_)) == enc-ex(enc-and(enc-in(ebvar(0), nth(min_ - mik_, mia_)), enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, mik_))))

enc-cch-zero

forall([mix_, mia_, min_], enc-cch(mix_, mia_, min_, 0) == enc-eq(mix_, enc-list(idx-list(min_))))

enc-eql-succ

forall([mcl_, mca_, mcn_ in nn], enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-and(enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, mcn_), enc-eq(nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_), nth(succ(mcn_), mca_))))

enc-eql-zero

forall([mcl_, mca_], enc-eql(mcl_, mca_, 0) == enc-truth)

enc-falsity-def

enc-falsity == eapp(1, [])

enc-nnc-def

enc-nnc == econst(3)

enc-ordc-def

enc-ordc == econst(5)

enc-orl-succ

forall([mcx_, mcl_, mcn_ in nn], enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, succ(mcn_)) == enc-or(enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, mcn_), enc-in(mcx_, nth(succ(mcn_), mcl_))))

enc-orl-zero

forall([mcx_, mcl_], enc-orl(mcx_, mcl_, 0) == enc-falsity)

enc-setc-def

enc-setc == econst(1)

enc-tcj-succ

forall([mia_, mib_, min_, mik_ in nn], enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, succ(mik_)) == if(mik_ = 0, enc-eq(nth(min_, mia_), nth(min_, mib_)), enc-and(enc-eq(nth(min_ - mik_, mia_), nth(min_ - mik_, mib_)), enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, mik_))))

enc-tcj-zero

forall([mia_, mib_, min_], enc-tcj(mia_, mib_, min_, 0) == enc-truth)

enc-truth-def

enc-truth == eapp(0, [])

eplus-def

eplus == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr-pos-star, rr-pos-star), if(x_ = pos-inf or y_ = pos-inf, pos-inf, binplus(x_, y_)))

etimes-def

etimes == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr-pos-star, rr-pos-star), if(x_ = pos-inf or y_ = pos-inf, if(x_ = 0 or y_ = 0, 0, pos-inf), bintimes(x_, y_)))

even-def

forall([x], even(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k))

expr-set-def

expr-set == big-union(mxn_, nn, expr-stage(mxn_))

expr-stage-succ

forall([mxn_ in nn], expr-stage(succ(mxn_)) == union(expr-stage(mxn_), union(cartesian(pair(3, 3), nn, tuples(expr-stage(mxn_))), cartesian(pair(4, 4), nn, nn, tuples(expr-stage(mxn_)), expr-stage(mxn_)))))

expr-stage-zero

expr-stage(0) == union(cartesian(pair(0, 0), nn), union(cartesian(pair(1, 1), nn), cartesian(pair(2, 2), nn)))

factorial-succ

forall([k in nn], factorial(succ(k)) = succ(k) * factorial(k))

factorial-zero

factorial(0) = succ(0)

falling-succ

forall([n, k in nn], falling(n, succ(k)) == (n - k) * falling(n, k))

falling-zero

forall([n], falling(n, 0) == succ(0))

fam-of-list-apply

forall([l, i], i in nn and succ(i) <= length(l) implies (fam-of-list(l))(i) = nth(succ(i), l))

fbs-v-add-sub-rev-sip-as-module

fbs-v-add-sub-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

fbs-v-add-sub-rev-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

fbs-v-add-sub-sip-as-module

fbs-v-add-sub-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

fbs-v-sub-add-rev-sip-as-module

fbs-v-sub-add-rev-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

fbs-v-sub-add-sip-as-module

fbs-v-sub-add-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

field-non-zero-carrier

finsum-all-id-rev-ring-additive-ag

finsum-all-id-ring-additive-ag

finsum-single-support-rev-ring-additive-ag

finsum-single-support-ring-additive-ag

forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r)), i0 in s], forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies f(j) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = f(i0))))

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), z_ in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(x_, (vadd(r))(y_, z_)) = (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(x_, y_), z_)))

hbs-vadd-comm-rev-sip-as-module

forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(y_, x_) = (vadd(r))(x_, y_)))

hbs-vadd-comm-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(y_, x_) = (vadd(r))(x_, y_)))

imag-part-def

forall([z], imag-part(z) == (z - conjugate(z)) * recip(2 * 1i))

image-membership-iff

forall([phi, s, w], w in image(phi, s) iff w in set and forsome([x in s], phi(x) = w))

inf-subsets-membership

forall([a, s], s in inf-subsets(a) iff s subset a and not(card(s) in nn))

injection-membership-iff

forall([x, y, phi], phi in injection(x, y) iff phi in fun(x, y) and forall([a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b))

ip-normed-ag-carrier

forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies carr(ip-normed-ag(v)) == vec(v))

ip-normed-ag-norm-value

forall([v, x in vec(v)], (nrm(ip-normed-ag(v)))(x) == ip-norm(v, x))

is-bounded-linear-arrow-def

forall([a, b, f], is-bounded-linear-arrow(a, b, f) iff is-normed-vector-space(a) and is-normed-vector-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forsome([blk_ in rr], 0 <= blk_ and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], (vnrm(b))(f(x1_)) <= blk_ * (vnrm(a))(x1_))))

forall([a, b, f1, f2, f3], is-hom-strand-space(a, b, f1, f2, f3) iff is-strand-space(a) and is-strand-space(b) and f1 in fun(tokens(a), tokens(b)) and f2 in fun(ids(a), ids(b)) and f3 in fun(keys(a), keys(b)) and forall([x1_ in ids(a)], (strand-map(a))(x1_) = (strand-map(b))(f2(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in keys(a)], f3((kinv(a))(x1_)) = (kinv(b))(f3(x1_))))

is-hom-top-space-def

forall([s, t, f], is-hom-top-space(s, t, f) iff is-top-space(s) and is-top-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in opens(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in opens(s)))

is-hom-vector-space-def

forall([a, b, f], is-hom-vector-space(a, b, f) iff is-vector-space(a) and is-vector-space(b) and is-hom-module(a, b, f))

is-lipschitz-arrow-def

forall([a, b, f], is-lipschitz-arrow(a, b, f) iff is-metric-space(a) and is-metric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forsome([lpk_ in rr], 0 <= lpk_ and forall([lpx_ in pts(a), lpy_ in pts(a)], (dist(b))(f(lpx_), f(lpy_)) <= lpk_ * (dist(a))(lpx_, lpy_))))

is-ord

forall([x], is-ord(x) iff x in ord)

is-set

forall([x], is-set(x) iff x in set)

is-uniformly-continuous-arrow-def

forall([a, b, f], is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, b, f) iff is-metric-space(a) and is-metric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and is-uniformly-continuous(a, b, f))

keep-set-membership

forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha, y_], y_ in keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha) iff y_ in grd and y_ = phi(alpha) and forall([z in kset], [z, y_] in porel))

lastcoeff-set-membership

forall([md, p, u, sm, r_], r_ in lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm) iff r_ in carr(scal(md)) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c_, 1, succ(p)) = r_ and lincomb(md, succ(p), c_, u) in sm))

lcd-stage-succ

forall([mxn_ in nn], lcd-stage(succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(3, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_))}, {mxd_ in nn: forall([mxw_ in make-set(nth(4, mxe_))], mxd_ in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(mxw_)) and mxd_ + nth(3, mxe_) in (lcd-stage(mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))}))))

lcd-stage-zero

lcd-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 1, {mxd_ in nn: nth(2, mxe_) < mxd_}, nn))

length-cons

forall([x, l], x in set implies length(cons(x, l)) = succ(length(l)))

lft-group-def

lft-group = [lfts, vnb-lambda([lfg_, lfh_], cartesian(lfts, lfts), compose(lfg_, lfh_)), id-fun(cc-hat), vnb-lambda(lfg_, lfts, inverse-bij(lfg_, cc-hat, cc-hat))]

lft-group@carr

carr(lft-group) == lfts

lft-group@iden

iden(lft-group) == id-fun(cc-hat)

lft-group@inv

inv(lft-group) == vnb-lambda(lfg_, lfts, inverse-bij(lfg_, cc-hat, cc-hat))

lft-group@opr

opr(lft-group) == vnb-lambda([lfg_, lfh_], cartesian(lfts, lfts), compose(lfg_, lfh_))

lfts-def

lfts == {lff_ in fun(cc-hat, cc-hat): forsome([lfa_ in cc, lfb_ in cc, lfc_ in cc, lfd_ in cc], not(lfa_ * lfd_ - lfb_ * lfc_ = 0) and lff_ = lft(lfa_, lfb_, lfc_, lfd_))}

limit-ord-iff

forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) iff lambda in ord and not(lambda = 0) and not(forsome([alpha in ord], lambda = succ_ord(alpha))))

magnitude-def

forall([z], magnitude(z) == sqrt(real-part(z) * real-part(z) + imag-part(z) * imag-part(z)))

makeset-cons

forall([x, l], x in set implies make-set(cons(x, l)) = union(pair(x, x), make-set(l)))

matact-assoc-rev-sip-as-module

forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(r))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(r))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(r)) implies (n = 0 implies m = 0 or k = 0 and l = 0) implies (k = 0 implies l = 0 or n = 0 and m = 0) implies matact(sip-as-module(r), p, matact(sip-as-module(r), q, u)) = matact(sip-as-module(r), matmul(scal(r), p, q), u)))

matrix-membership

forall([s, m], m in matrix(s) iff m in tuples(tuples(s)) and forall([i], i in nn and 1 <= i and i <= length(m) implies forall([j], j in nn and 1 <= j and j <= length(m) implies length(nth(i, m)) = length(nth(j, m)))))

module-act-distrib-scalar

forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_))))

forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), j in nn, k in nn], mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j + k) = (opr(r))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j), mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, k))))

mpow-succ

forall([m, x, n in nn], mpow(m, x, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(x, mpow(m, x, n)))

mpow-zero

forall([m, x], mpow(m, x, 0) == iden(m))

msg-stage-succ

forall([smt_, smk_, smn_ in nn], msg-stage(smt_, smk_, succ(smn_)) == union(msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_), union(cartesian(pair(1, 1), tuples(msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_))), cartesian(pair(2, 2), msg-stage(smt_, smk_, smn_), smk_))))

msg-stage-zero

forall([smt_, smk_], msg-stage(smt_, smk_, 0) == smt_)

nary-minus-2

forall([x, y], x - y == binplus(x, binneg(y)))

nary-neg-1

forall([x], -x == binneg(x))

nary-plus-2

forall([x, y], x + y == binplus(x, y))

nary-plus-3

forall([x, y, z], x + y + z == binplus(binplus(x, y), z))

nary-plus-4

forall([w, x, y, z], w + x + y + z == binplus(binplus(binplus(w, x), y), z))

nary-plus-5

forall([v, w, x, y, z], v + w + x + y + z == binplus(binplus(binplus(binplus(v, w), x), y), z))

nary-times-2

forall([x, y], x * y == bintimes(x, y))

nary-times-3

forall([x, y, z], x * y * z == bintimes(bintimes(x, y), z))

nary-times-4

forall([w, x, y, z], w * x * y * z == bintimes(bintimes(bintimes(w, x), y), z))

nary-times-5

forall([v, w, x, y, z], v * w * x * y * z == bintimes(bintimes(bintimes(bintimes(v, w), x), y), z))

nf-metric-carrier

forall([nf], pts(nf-metric-space(nf)) == carr(nf))

nf-metric-distance

forall([nf], is-normed-field(nf) implies forall([x in carr(nf), y in carr(nf)], (dist(nf-metric-space(nf)))(x, y) = (fnrm(nf))((add(nf))(x, (neg(nf))(y)))))

nn-add-monoid-def

nn-add-monoid = [nn, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(nn, nn), x_ + y_), 0]

nn-add-monoid@carr

carr(nn-add-monoid) == nn

nn-add-monoid@iden

iden(nn-add-monoid) == 0

nn-add-monoid@opr

opr(nn-add-monoid) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(nn, nn), x_ + y_)

nn-add-succ

forall([a in nn, b in nn], a + succ(b) = succ(a + b))

nn-minus-def

forall([k in nn, l in nn], nn-minus(k, l) = if(l <= k, k - l, 0))

nth-cons-1

forall([x, l], x in set implies nth(1, cons(x, l)) = x)

nth-cons-succ

forall([i, x, l], x in set implies i in nn and 1 <= i and i <= length(l) implies nth(succ(i), cons(x, l)) = nth(i, l))

nth-deriv-on-succ

forall([ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, ndvn_ in nn], nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, succ(ndvn_)) == vnb-lambda(ndvz_, ndvu_, deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, ndvn_), ndvz_)))

nth-deriv-on-zero

forall([ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_], nth-deriv-on(ndvk_, ndvu_, ndvf_, 0) == ndvf_)

nth-deriv-succ

forall([f, n in nn], nth-deriv(f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(nth-deriv(f, n), x)))

nth-deriv-v-succ

forall([m, f, n in nn], nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, n), x)))

nth-deriv-v-zero

forall([m, f], nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0) == f)

nth-deriv-zero

forall([f], nth-deriv(f, 0) == f)

odd-def

forall([x], odd(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k + 1))

open-stage-succ

forall([mxu_, mxn_ in nn], open-stage(mxu_, succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda([mxe_, mxk_], cartesian(expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), nn), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (open-stage(mxu_, mxn_))(mxe_, mxk_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, [3, nth(2, mxe_), maplk(open-stage(mxu_, mxn_), nth(3, mxe_), mxk_)], [4, nth(2, mxe_), nth(3, mxe_), maplk(open-stage(mxu_, mxn_), nth(4, mxe_), mxk_), (open-stage(mxu_, mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_), mxk_ + nth(3, mxe_))]))))

open-stage-zero

forall([mxu_], open-stage(mxu_, 0) == vnb-lambda([mxe_, mxk_], cartesian(expr-stage(0), nn), if(mxe_ = [1, mxk_], mxu_, mxe_)))

ord-lt-iff

forall([alpha, beta], ord-lt(alpha, beta) iff ord-le(alpha, beta) and not(alpha = beta))

pi-def

pi == iota(cekp_, cekp_ in rr and 0 < cekp_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekp_) * 1i))))

preimage-membership

forall([s, f, v, a], a in preimage(s, f, v) iff a in pts(s) and f(a) in v)

principal-ideal-membership

forall([s, a, x], x in principal-ideal(s, a) iff x in carr(s) and forsome([r in carr(s)], x = (mul(s))(r, a)))

prod-ord-succ

forall([m, f, n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(prod-ord(m, f, n), f(n)))

prod-ord-zero

forall([m, f], prod-ord(m, f, 0) == iden(m))

qq-field-def

qq-field = [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1, difference(qq, singleton(0)), vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_))]

qq-field@add

add(qq-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)

qq-field@carr

carr(qq-field) == qq

qq-field@mul

mul(qq-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_)

qq-field@mul-inv

mul-inv(qq-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_))

qq-field@neg

neg(qq-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)

qq-field@non-zero

non-zero(qq-field) == difference(qq, singleton(0))

qq-field@one

one(qq-field) == 1

qq-field@zero

zero(qq-field) == 0

qq-line-def

qq-line = [field-as-integral-domain(qq-field), qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), 0, vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(qq, qq), r_ * x_)]

qq-line@act

act(qq-line) == vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(qq, qq), r_ * x_)

qq-line@scal

scal(qq-line) == field-as-integral-domain(qq-field)

qq-line@vadd

vadd(qq-line) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)

qq-line@vec

vec(qq-line) == qq

qq-line@vneg

vneg(qq-line) == vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)

qq-line@vzero

vzero(qq-line) == 0

qq-ring-def

qq-ring = [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1]

qq-ring@add

add(qq-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)

qq-ring@carr

carr(qq-ring) == qq

qq-ring@mul

mul(qq-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_)

qq-ring@neg

neg(qq-ring) == vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)

qq-ring@one

one(qq-ring) == 1

qq-ring@zero

zero(qq-ring) == 0

forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(r) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) implies forall([rfn in fun(vec(r), rr)], forall([rax_ in vec(r), ray_ in vec(r)], rfn((vadd(r))(rax_, ray_)) = rfn(rax_) + rfn(ray_)) implies forall([rhr_ in rr, rhx_ in vec(r)], rfn((act(r))(rhr_, rhx_)) = rhr_ * rfn(rhx_)) implies forall([cfn in fun(vec(r), cc)], forall([cvy_ in vec(r)], cfn(cvy_) == rfn(cvy_) + -rfn((act(r))(1i, cvy_)) * 1i) implies forall([a_ in rr, b_ in rr, x in vec(r)], (a_ + 1i * b_) * cfn(x) = cfn((act(r))(a_ + 1i * b_, x))))))

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(r) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) implies forall([rfn in fun(vec(r), rr)], forall([rax_ in vec(r), ray_ in vec(r)], rfn((vadd(r))(rax_, ray_)) = rfn(rax_) + rfn(ray_)) implies forall([rhr_ in rr, rhx_ in vec(r)], rfn((act(r))(rhr_, rhx_)) = rhr_ * rfn(rhx_)) implies forall([cfn in fun(vec(r), cc)], forall([cvy_ in vec(r)], cfn(cvy_) == rfn(cvy_) + -rfn((act(r))(1i, cvy_)) * 1i) implies forall([r_ in rr, x in vec(r)], cfn((act(r))(r_, x)) = r_ * cfn(x)))))

rb3-cf-rhom-sip-as-module-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(r) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) implies forall([rfn in fun(vec(r), rr)], forall([rax_ in vec(r), ray_ in vec(r)], rfn((vadd(r))(rax_, ray_)) = rfn(rax_) + rfn(ray_)) implies forall([rhr_ in rr, rhx_ in vec(r)], rfn((act(r))(rhr_, rhx_)) = rhr_ * rfn(rhx_)) implies forall([cfn in fun(vec(r), cc)], forall([cvy_ in vec(r)], cfn(cvy_) == rfn(cvy_) + -rfn((act(r))(1i, cvy_)) * 1i) implies forall([r_ in rr, x in vec(r)], r_ * cfn(x) = cfn((act(r))(r_, x))))))

rb3-mr-exists-sip-as-module

forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(r) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) implies forsome([mr], is-module(mr) and scal(mr) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) and vec(mr) = vec(r) and vadd(mr) = vadd(r) and vzero(mr) = vzero(r) and vneg(mr) = vneg(r) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(r)], (act(mr))(r_, x_) = (act(r))(r_, x_))))

rb3-mr-exists-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip

rb4-lincomb-trunc-rev-sip-as-module

real-part-def

forall([z], real-part(z) == (z + conjugate(z)) * recip(2))

received-def

received = 1

reduce-one

forall([op, f], reduce(op, f, 1) == f(0))

reduce-succ

forall([op, f, n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies reduce(op, f, succ(n)) == op(reduce(op, f, n), f(n)))

ring-add-assoc

forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (add(s))((add(s))(a, b), c) = (add(s))(a, (add(s))(b, c))))

ring-add-assoc-rev-ringoid-as-ring

forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, (add(r))(b, c)) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), c)))

ring-add-assoc-ringoid-as-ring

forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), c) = (add(r))(a, (add(r))(b, c))))

ring-add-comm

forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, b) = (add(s))(b, a)))

ring-add-left-id-ringoid-as-ring

forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(zero(r), a) = a))

forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))((neg(r))(a), a) = zero(r)))

ring-prod-n-succ

forall([f, n in nn], ring-prod-n(f, succ(n)) == ring-prod(ring-prod-n(f, n), f(n)))

ring-prod-n-zero

forall([f], ring-prod-n(f, 0) == zero-ring)

ring-right-dist

forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (mul(s))((add(s))(a, b), c) = (add(s))((mul(s))(a, c), (mul(s))(b, c))))

ring-right-dist-ringoid-as-ring

ringoid-add-right-inv

forall([r, a], is-ringoid(r) implies a in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(a)) = zero(r))

ringoid-rel-mem

forall([r, a, b], [a, b] in ringoid-rel(r) iff [a, b] in cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)) and (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r))

ringoid-setoid-pts

forall([r], pts(ringoid-setoid(r)) == carr(r))

rm-two-def

rm-two == pair(0, 1)

rm-two-ms-def

rm-two-ms == subspace-ms(rr-ms, rm-two)

rn-maxabs-succ

forall([rnmx_, rnmk_ in nn], rn-maxabs(rnmx_, succ(rnmk_)) == max(rn-maxabs(rnmx_, rnmk_), abs(rnmx_(succ(rnmk_)))))

rn-maxabs-zero

forall([rnmx_], rn-maxabs(rnmx_, 0) == 0)

rpow-def

forall([a_, b_], rpow(a_, b_) == rpow-star(a_, b_))

rq-add

forall([r], add(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))))

rq-carr

forall([r], carr(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == quotient(ringoid-setoid(r)))

rq-mul

forall([r], mul(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b)))))

rq-neg

forall([r], neg(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == descend(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a)))))

rq-one

forall([r], one(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == class(ringoid-setoid(r), one(r)))

rq-zero

forall([r], zero(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == class(ringoid-setoid(r), zero(r)))

rr-abs-def

forall([x in rr], (0 <= x implies abs(x) = x) and (not(0 <= x) implies abs(x) = -x))

rr-bounded-ms-def

rr-bounded-ms == bdd-metric(rr-ms)

rr-max-def

forall([x in rr, y in rr], (y <= x implies max(x, y) = x) and (not(y <= x) implies max(x, y) = y))

rr-min-def

forall([x in rr, y in rr], (x <= y implies min(x, y) = x) and (not(x <= y) implies min(x, y) = y))

rr-ms-def

rr-ms = [rr, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(rr, rr), abs(x - y))]

rr-ms@dist

dist(rr-ms) == vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(rr, rr), abs(x - y))

rr-ms@pts

pts(rr-ms) == rr

rr-normed-field-def

rr-normed-field = [rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))]

rr-normed-field@add

add(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)

rr-normed-field@carr

carr(rr-normed-field) == rr

rr-normed-field@fnrm

fnrm(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))

rr-normed-field@mul

mul(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_)

rr-normed-field@neg

neg(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)

rr-normed-field@one

one(rr-normed-field) == 1

rr-normed-field@zero

zero(rr-normed-field) == 0

rr-nvs-def

rr-nvs = [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), 0, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(rr, rr), r_ * x_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))]

rr-nvs@act

act(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(rr, rr), r_ * x_)

rr-nvs@scal

scal(rr-nvs) == normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)

rr-nvs@vadd

vadd(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)

rr-nvs@vec

vec(rr-nvs) == rr

rr-nvs@vneg

vneg(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)

rr-nvs@vnrm

vnrm(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))

rr-nvs@vzero

vzero(rr-nvs) == 0

rr-pos-star-add-monoid-def

rr-pos-star-add-monoid = [rr-pos-star, eplus, 0]

rr-pos-star-add-monoid@carr

carr(rr-pos-star-add-monoid) == rr-pos-star

rr-pos-star-add-monoid@iden

iden(rr-pos-star-add-monoid) == 0

rr-pos-star-add-monoid@opr

opr(rr-pos-star-add-monoid) == eplus

rr-pos-star-membership

forall([x], x in rr-pos-star iff x in rr and 0 <= x or x = pos-inf)

rr-star-membership

forall([x], x in rr-star iff x in rr or x = pos-inf or x = neg-inf)

rw-univ-def

rw-univ == cartesian(nn, ctx-set, expr-set, expr-set)

sent-def

sent = 0

seq-def

seq-set == cartesian(ctx-set, clf)

sigma-generated-membership

forall([omega, ce, a_], a_ in sigma-generated(omega, ce) iff a_ in power(omega) and forall([cb_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, cb_) and ce subset cb_ implies a_ in cb_))

sip-act-neg-scalar-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([q_ in rr, x_ in vec(r)], (act(r))(-q_, x_) = (vneg(r))((act(r))(q_, x_))))

sip-act-neg-scalar-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([q_ in rr, x_ in vec(r)], (vneg(r))((act(r))(q_, x_)) = (act(r))(-q_, x_)))

sip-class-eq-iff-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r), b_ in vec(r)], class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_) = class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), b_) iff (rip(r))((vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)), (vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_))) = 0))

sip-class-eq-iff-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r), b_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))((vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)), (vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_))) = 0 iff class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_) = class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), b_)))

sip-expand-two-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), a_ in rr, b_ in rr], (rip(r))((vadd(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), (act(r))(b_, y_)), (vadd(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), (act(r))(b_, y_))) = ((a_ * a_) * (rip(r))(x_, x_) + (2 * (a_ * b_)) * (rip(r))(x_, y_)) + (b_ * b_) * (rip(r))(y_, y_)))

sip-expand-two-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), a_ in rr, b_ in rr], ((a_ * a_) * (rip(r))(x_, x_) + (2 * (a_ * b_)) * (rip(r))(x_, y_)) + (b_ * b_) * (rip(r))(y_, y_) = (rip(r))((vadd(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), (act(r))(b_, y_)), (vadd(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), (act(r))(b_, y_)))))

sip-null-act-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies (rip(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), (act(r))(a_, x_)) = 0))

sip-null-act-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies 0 = (rip(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), (act(r))(a_, x_))))

sip-null-orth-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies forall([y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, y_) = 0)))

sip-null-orth-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies forall([y_ in vec(r)], 0 = (rip(r))(x_, y_))))

sip-null-out-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in sip-null(csip-as-sip(r))], x_ in vec(r) and (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0))

sip-null-vadd-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies (rip(r))(y_, y_) = 0 implies (rip(r))((vadd(r))(x_, y_), (vadd(r))(x_, y_)) = 0))

sip-null-vadd-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies (rip(r))(y_, y_) = 0 implies 0 = (rip(r))((vadd(r))(x_, y_), (vadd(r))(x_, y_))))

sip-null-vneg-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies (rip(r))((vneg(r))(x_), (vneg(r))(x_)) = 0))

sip-null-vneg-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, x_) = 0 implies 0 = (rip(r))((vneg(r))(x_), (vneg(r))(x_))))

sip-rel-out-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_, b_], [a_, b_] in sip-rel(csip-as-sip(r)) implies (rip(r))((vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)), (vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_))) = 0))

sip-rel-out-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_, b_], [a_, b_] in sip-rel(csip-as-sip(r)) implies 0 = (rip(r))((vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)), (vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)))))

sip-related-out-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_, b_], related(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_, b_) implies (rip(r))((vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)), (vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_))) = 0))

sip-related-out-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_, b_], related(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_, b_) implies 0 = (rip(r))((vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)), (vadd(r))(a_, (vneg(r))(b_)))))

sip-rip-add-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), z_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))((vadd(r))(x_, y_), z_) = (rip(r))(x_, z_) + (rip(r))(y_, z_)))

sip-rip-add-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), z_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, z_) + (rip(r))(y_, z_) = (rip(r))((vadd(r))(x_, y_), z_)))

sip-rip-add-right-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), z_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, (vadd(r))(y_, z_)) = (rip(r))(x_, y_) + (rip(r))(x_, z_)))

sip-rip-add-right-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r), z_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, y_) + (rip(r))(x_, z_) = (rip(r))(x_, (vadd(r))(y_, z_))))

sip-rip-homog-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (rip(r))(x_, y_)))

sip-rip-homog-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], a_ * (rip(r))(x_, y_) = (rip(r))((act(r))(a_, x_), y_)))

sip-rip-homog-right-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, (act(r))(a_, y_)) = a_ * (rip(r))(x_, y_)))

sip-rip-homog-right-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], a_ * (rip(r))(x_, y_) = (rip(r))(x_, (act(r))(a_, y_))))

sip-rip-neg-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))((vneg(r))(x_), y_) = -(rip(r))(x_, y_)))

sip-rip-neg-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], -(rip(r))(x_, y_) = (rip(r))((vneg(r))(x_), y_)))

sip-rip-neg-right-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, (vneg(r))(y_)) = -(rip(r))(x_, y_)))

sip-rip-neg-right-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], -(rip(r))(x_, y_) = (rip(r))(x_, (vneg(r))(y_))))

sip-rip-respects-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), u_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))((vadd(r))(x_, (vneg(r))(u_)), (vadd(r))(x_, (vneg(r))(u_))) = 0 implies (rip(r))(x_, y_) = (rip(r))(u_, y_)))

sip-rip-respects-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), u_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))((vadd(r))(x_, (vneg(r))(u_)), (vadd(r))(x_, (vneg(r))(u_))) = 0 implies (rip(r))(u_, y_) = (rip(r))(x_, y_)))

sip-rip-sym-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, y_) = (rip(r))(y_, x_)))

sip-rip-sym-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(y_, x_) = (rip(r))(x_, y_)))

sip-rip-zero-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([y_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(vzero(r), y_) = 0))

sip-rip-zero-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([y_ in vec(r)], 0 = (rip(r))(vzero(r), y_)))

sip-rip-zero-right-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(x_, vzero(r)) = 0))

sip-rip-zero-right-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(r)], 0 = (rip(r))(x_, vzero(r))))

sip-scal-carr-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies carr(scal(r)) = rr)

sip-scal-carr-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies rr = carr(scal(r)))

sip-scal-pin-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(r) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field))

sip-scal-pin-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) = scal(r))

sip-setoid-pts-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies pts(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))) == vec(r))

sip-setoid-pts-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies vec(r) == pts(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))))

sip-setoid-rel-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies rel(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))) == sip-rel(csip-as-sip(r)))

sip-setoid-rel-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies sip-rel(csip-as-sip(r)) == rel(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-act-computes-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in rr, a_ in vec(r)], (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_)) = class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (act(r))(r_, a_))))

sipq-act-computes-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in rr, a_ in vec(r)], class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (act(r))(r_, a_)) = (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_))))

sipq-act-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == sip-qact(csip-as-sip(r)))

sipq-act-distrib-scalar-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), s_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((add(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, x_), (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(s_, x_))))

sipq-act-distrib-scalar-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), s_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, x_), (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(s_, x_)) = (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((add(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))))(r_, s_), x_)))

sipq-act-distrib-vec-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, x_), (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, y_))))

sipq-act-distrib-vec-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, x_), (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, y_)) = (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_))))

sipq-act-mul-compat-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), s_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((mul(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(s_, x_))))

sipq-act-mul-compat-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), s_ in carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(r_, (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(s_, x_)) = (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((mul(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))))(r_, s_), x_)))

sipq-act-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies sip-qact(csip-as-sip(r)) == act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-act-unital-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(one(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_) = x_))

sipq-act-unital-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], x_ = (act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(one(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))), x_)))

sipq-definite-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))))

sipq-definite-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, x_) = 0 implies vzero(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) = x_))

sipq-length-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies length(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) = 7)

sipq-length-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies 7 = length(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-rip-add-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), z_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_), z_) = (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, z_) + (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(y_, z_)))

sipq-rip-add-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), z_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, z_) + (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(y_, z_) = (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_), z_)))

sipq-rip-computes-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r), b_ in vec(r)], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), b_)) = (rip(r))(a_, b_)))

sipq-rip-computes-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r), b_ in vec(r)], (rip(r))(a_, b_) = (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), b_))))

sipq-rip-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == sip-qrip(csip-as-sip(r)))

sipq-rip-homog-left-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_)))

sipq-rip-homog-left-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], a_ * (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_) = (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))((act(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(a_, x_), y_)))

sipq-rip-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies sip-qrip(csip-as-sip(r)) == rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-rip-sym-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_) = (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(y_, x_)))

sipq-rip-sym-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))], (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(y_, x_) = (rip(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(x_, y_)))

sipq-scal-carr-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))) = rr)

sipq-scal-carr-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies rr = carr(scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))))

sipq-scal-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == scal(r))

sipq-scal-pin-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field))

sipq-scal-pin-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) = scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-scal-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies scal(r) == scal(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-scale-computes-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in rr, a_ in vec(r)], (sip-qscale(csip-as-sip(r), r_))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_)) = class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (act(r))(r_, a_))))

sipq-scale-computes-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in rr, a_ in vec(r)], class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (act(r))(r_, a_)) = (sip-qscale(csip-as-sip(r), r_))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_))))

sipq-unfold-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)) == [scal(r), quotient(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))), sip-qvadd(csip-as-sip(r)), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), vzero(r)), sip-qvneg(csip-as-sip(r)), sip-qact(csip-as-sip(r)), sip-qrip(csip-as-sip(r))])

sipq-unfold-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies [scal(r), quotient(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))), sip-qvadd(csip-as-sip(r)), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), vzero(r)), sip-qvneg(csip-as-sip(r)), sip-qact(csip-as-sip(r)), sip-qrip(csip-as-sip(r))] == sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r)))

sipq-vadd-computes-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r), b_ in vec(r)], (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), b_)) = class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (vadd(r))(a_, b_))))

sipq-vadd-computes-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r), b_ in vec(r)], class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (vadd(r))(a_, b_)) = (vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_), class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), b_))))

sipq-vadd-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == sip-qvadd(csip-as-sip(r)))

sipq-vadd-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies sip-qvadd(csip-as-sip(r)) == vadd(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-vec-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == quotient(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-vec-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies quotient(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r))) == vec(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-vneg-computes-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r)], (vneg(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_)) = class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (vneg(r))(a_))))

sipq-vneg-computes-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a_ in vec(r)], class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), (vneg(r))(a_)) = (vneg(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))(class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), a_))))

sipq-vneg-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies vneg(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == sip-qvneg(csip-as-sip(r)))

sipq-vneg-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies sip-qvneg(csip-as-sip(r)) == vneg(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

sipq-vzero-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies vzero(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))) == class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), vzero(r)))

sipq-vzero-rev-csip-as-sip

forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies class(sip-setoid(csip-as-sip(r)), vzero(r)) == vzero(sip-quotient(csip-as-sip(r))))

span-membership

forall([md, n, u, x_], x_ in span(md, n, u) iff x_ in vec(md) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = lincomb(md, n, c_, u)))

sqn-membership

forall([a, f_], f_ in sqn(a) iff f_ in fun(nn, a))

subst-stage-succ

forall([mxm_, mxu_, mxn_ in nn], subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, succ(mxn_)) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(succ(mxn_)), if(mxe_ in expr-stage(mxn_), (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_))(mxe_), if(nth(1, mxe_) = 3, [3, nth(2, mxe_), mapl(subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_), nth(3, mxe_))], [4, nth(2, mxe_), nth(3, mxe_), mapl(subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_), nth(4, mxe_)), (subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, mxn_))(nth(5, mxe_))]))))

subst-stage-zero

forall([mxm_, mxu_], subst-stage(mxm_, mxu_, 0) == vnb-lambda(mxe_, expr-stage(0), if(mxe_ = [0, mxm_], mxu_, mxe_)))

sum-ag-as-reduce-ring-additive-ag

forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, n) = reduce(add(r), f, n)))

sum-ag-singleton-ring-additive-ag

forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r))], sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, 1) = f(0)))

sum-ag-succ

forall([ag, f, n in nn], sum-ag(ag, f, succ(n)) == (opr(ag))(sum-ag(ag, f, n), f(n)))

sum-ag-zero

forall([ag, f], sum-ag(ag, f, 0) == iden(ag))

sum-succ

forall([r, f, n in nn], sum(r, f, succ(n)) == (add(r))(sum(r, f, n), f(n)))

sum-zero

forall([r, f], sum(r, f, 0) == zero(r))

taylor-poly-v-succ

forall([m, f, a, x, n in nn], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, succ(n)) == (vadd(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n), (act(m))((x - a) ^ succ(n) * recip(factorial(succ(n))), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(a))))

taylor-poly-v-zero

forall([m, f, a, x], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, 0) == (act(m))((x - a) ^ 0 * recip(factorial(0)), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0))(a)))

trinum-succ

forall([n_ in nn], trinum(succ(n_)) == trinum(n_) + succ(n_))

trinum-zero

trinum(0) == 0

unit-circle-def

unit-circle == {ucz_ in cc: magnitude(ucz_) = 1}

upper-half-plane-def

upper-half-plane == {hpz_ in cc: 0 < imag-part(hpz_)}

wks-stage-succ

forall([mcn_ in nn], wks-stage(succ(mcn_)) == vnb-lambda(mce_, expr-stage(succ(mcn_)), if(mce_ in expr-stage(mcn_), (wks-stage(mcn_))(mce_), if(nth(1, mce_) = 3, {mckk_ in nn: mckk_ = hd-res(nth(2, mce_)) and app-ar-ok(nth(2, mce_), length(nth(3, mce_))) and forall([mcw_ in make-set(nth(3, mce_))], hd-argk(nth(2, mce_)) in (wks-stage(mcn_))(mcw_))}, {mckk_ in nn: mckk_ = hd-bres(nth(2, mce_)) and bind-sig(nth(2, mce_), nth(3, mce_), length(nth(4, mce_))) and forall([mcw_ in make-set(nth(4, mce_))], 1 in (wks-stage(mcn_))(mcw_)) and hd-body(nth(2, mce_)) in (wks-stage(mcn_))(nth(5, mce_))}))))

wks-stage-zero

wks-stage(0) == vnb-lambda(mce_, expr-stage(0), pair(1, 1))

zen-limit

forall([x, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zen(x, lam) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))

zen-succ

forall([x, alpha in ord], zen(x, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-le(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))

zen-zero

forall([x], zen(x, 0) = choice(x))

zero-ring-def

zero-ring = [{0}, vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda(p, {0}, 0), 0, 0]

zkept-limit

forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, lam) = big-union(beta, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta)))

zkept-succ

forall([phi, grd, porel, alpha in ord], zkept(phi, grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = union(zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), keep-set(phi, grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), alpha)))

zkept-zero

forall([phi, grd, porel], zkept(phi, grd, porel, 0) = empty-set)

zup-limit

forall([grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zup(grd, porel, lam) = choice({y_ in grd: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies [zup(grd, porel, c_), y_] in porel)}))

zup-succ

forall([grd, porel, alpha in ord], zup(grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in grd: is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, alpha), y_)}))

zup-zero

forall([grd, porel], zup(grd, porel, 0) = choice(grd))

zz-act-assoc-ring-additive-ag

forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j * k, a) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))

zz-act-distrib-ring-additive-ag

forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, (add(r))(a, b)) = (add(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a), zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, b))))

zz-bezout-set-membership

forall([a, b, z_], z_ in zz-bezout-set(a, b) iff z_ in zz and forsome([x_ in zz, y_ in zz], z_ = x_ * a + y_ * b))

zz-ring-def

zz-ring = [zz, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_), 0, 1]

zz-ring@add

add(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_)

zz-ring@carr

carr(zz-ring) == zz

zz-ring@mul

mul(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_)

zz-ring@neg

neg(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_)

zz-ring@one

one(zz-ring) == 1

zz-ring@zero

zero(zz-ring) == 0
